要有配套的docs-src以及tests/，对了多框架支持，比如aiohttp.web，全面支持一切

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
WARP 双实例高可用 HTTP 代理集群 v3.7

功能特性:
    - 双 WARP 实例运行在隔离的网络命名空间中
    - HTTP 代理负载均衡 (HAProxy)
    - 自动健康检查和故障恢复
    - 定时 IP 轮换 (支持无缝切换)
    - IP 去重: 自动检测两实例 IP 是否相同, 相同则重新注册 (最多 3 次)
    - reboot 后自动恢复: 通过 systemd 启动时重建命名空间和网络
    - 全彩色 ASCII 艺术终端界面
    - 交互式命令行 (ghost text 补全 + 命令历史)
    - 统一命令入口 warp
    - 支持 WARP+ 密钥配置
    - 多平台支持 (Debian/Ubuntu/CentOS/RHEL/Rocky/AlmaLinux/Fedora)

使用方式:
    warp                                        # 进入交互式命令行
    warp deploy                                 # 完整部署
    warp deploy --key1 xxx --key2 yyy           # 使用 WARP+ 密钥部署
    warp status                                 # 查看状态
    warp rotate                                 # 手动轮换
    warp test                                   # 测试代理
    warp cleanup                                # 完全清理
    warp monitor                                # 启动监控

版本: 3.7.0
"""

    'α': ['         ', '      █  ', ' ██████╗ ', '██╔══██╗ ', '╚██████║ ', ' ╚════█═╝'],
    'β': ['██████╗ ', '██╔══██╗', '██████╔╝', '██╔══██╗', '██████╔╝', '█═════╝ '],
    'γ': ['█     █   ', ' █   █    ', '███████╗  ', '╚══██╔══╝ ', '███╔╝     ', '╚══╝      '],
    'δ': [' ████╗   ', '██╔══██╗ ', '███║ ██║ ', '██║  ██║ ', '╚██████╔╝', ' ╚═════╝ '],
    'π': ['            ', '█████████╗  ', '╚══██╔═█═╝  ', '   ██║ █    ', '   ██║ ██   ', '   ╚═╝ ╚═╝  '],
    'Σ': ['███████╗', '██╔════╝', '█████╗  ', '██╔══╝  ', '███████╗', '╚══════╝'],
    'Ω': [' ██████╗ ', '██╔═══██╗', '██║   ██║', '╚██╗ ██╔╝', '   ╚═══╝ ', '         '],
    '∞': ['           ', '███╗ ███╗  ', '██████████║', '╚██╔═██╔══╝', ' ╚═╝ ╚═╝   ', '           '],
    '∑': ['███████╗', '██╔════╝', ' █████╗ ', '██╔══╝  ', '███████╗', '╚══════╝'],
    '∆': ['   ██╗   ', '  ████╗  ', ' ██╔═██╗ ', '██╔╝  ██╗', '████████╗', '╚═══════╝'],
    '√': ['      ██╗', '     ██╔╝', '    ██╔╝ ', '██ ██╔╝  ', '████╔╝   ', '╚══╝     '],
    '≤': ['   ██╗  ', '  ██╔╝  ', ' ██╔╝   ', '████╗   ', '╚██████╗', ' ╚═════╝'],
    '≥': ['██╗     ', '╚██╗    ', ' ╚██╗   ', ' ██████╗', '██████╔╝', '╚═════╝ '],
    '≠': ['     ██╗ ', '███████╗ ', '╚══██╔══╝', '███████╗ ', '██╔════╝ ', '╚═╝      '],
    '≈': ['         ', '███╗ ██╗ ', '╚██████╔╝', '███╗ ██╗ ', '╚██████╔╝', ' ╚═════╝ '],
    '∴': ['         ', ' ██╗     ', '████████╗', '╚══██╔══╝', '███╗██╗  ', '╚══╝╚═╝  '],
    '∵': ['███╗██╗  ', '╚══╝╚═╝  ', '████████╗', '╚══██╔══╝', ' ██╗     ', ' ╚═╝     '],
    '∈': ['         ', '██████╗  ', '██╔═══██╗', '██║ ████║', '╚██████╔╝', ' ╚═════╝ '],
    '∉': ['     ██╗   ', '██████╔╝   ', '██╔═█████╗ ', '██║██╔══██║', '╚██████╔╝  ', ' ╚═════╝   '],

# =============================================================================
# 内部工具
# =============================================================================

_ANSI_RE = re.compile(r"\033\[[0-9;]*[a-zA-Z]")


def _strip_ansi(text: str) -> str:
    """移除 ANSI 转义序列, 返回纯文本"""
    return _ANSI_RE.sub("", text)


def _safe_write(text: str) -> None:
    """安全地向 stdout 写入并刷新, 忽略管道断裂等错误"""
    try:
        sys.stdout.write(text)
        sys.stdout.flush()
    except (BrokenPipeError, IOError, OSError):
        pass


# =============================================================================
# 终端 UI
# =============================================================================


class UI:
    """终端界面渲染工具"""

    RESET = "\033[0m"
    BOLD = "\033[1m"
    DIM = "\033[2m"
    RED = "\033[31m"
    GREEN = "\033[32m"
    YELLOW = "\033[33m"
    BLUE = "\033[34m"
    MAGENTA = "\033[35m"
    CYAN = "\033[36m"
    WHITE = "\033[97m"

    @classmethod
    def _color_ok(cls) -> bool:
        """检测终端是否支持颜色"""
        try:
            return hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty") and sys.stdout.isatty()
        except Exception:
            return False

    @classmethod
    def c(cls, color: str, text: str) -> str:
        """对文本应用颜色"""
        if not cls._color_ok():
            return text
        return f"{color}{text}{cls.RESET}"

    @classmethod
    def banner(cls) -> str:
        """渲染 WARP ASCII 艺术 banner (对角线渐变)"""
        if CHAR_MAP:
            lines = ["", "", "", "", "", ""]
            for ch in "WARP":
                glyph = CHAR_MAP.get(ch, CHAR_MAP.get(ch.upper()))
                if glyph:
                    for i in range(6):
                        lines[i] += glyph[i]
        else:
            lines = [
                "██╗    ██╗ █████╗ ██████╗ ██████╗",
                "██║    ██║██╔══██╗██╔══██╗██╔══██╗",
                "██║ █╗ ██║███████║██████╔╝██████╔╝",
                "██║███╗██║██╔══██║██╔══██╗██╔═══╝",
                "╚███╔███╔╝██║  ██║██║  ██║██║",
                " ╚══╝╚══╝ ╚═╝  ╚═╝╚═╝  ╚═╝╚═╝",
            ]

        if not cls._color_ok():
            return "\n".join(f"  {line}" for line in lines)

        return cls._render_gradient_banner(lines)

    @classmethod
    def _render_gradient_banner(cls, lines: List[str]) -> str:
        """对角线渐变渲染 ASCII 艺术"""
        palette: List[Tuple[int, int, int]] = [
            (0, 200, 255),    # 亮蓝
            (0, 210, 200),    # 青
            (0, 200, 140),    # 青绿
            (0, 180, 100),    # 绿
            (0, 150, 60),     # 深绿
        ]

        height = len(lines)
        width = max(len(line) for line in lines) if lines else 0
        max_diag = width + height - 2 if (width + height) > 2 else 1

        result_lines: List[str] = []
        for row, line in enumerate(lines):
            chars: List[str] = []
            for col, ch in enumerate(line):
                if ch == " ":
                    chars.append(" ")
                    continue
                # 对角线因子: 左上角 0.0 -> 右下角 1.0
                factor = (col + row) / max_diag
                factor = max(0.0, min(1.0, factor))
                r, g, b = cls._multi_lerp(palette, factor)
                chars.append(f"\033[38;2;{r};{g};{b}m{ch}")
            chars.append(cls.RESET)
            result_lines.append("  " + "".join(chars))

        return "\n".join(result_lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _multi_lerp(
        colors: List[Tuple[int, int, int]], factor: float
    ) -> Tuple[int, int, int]:
        """多色渐变插值"""
        if not colors:
            return (255, 255, 255)
        if len(colors) == 1:
            return colors[0]
        factor = max(0.0, min(1.0, factor))
        seg = len(colors) - 1
        scaled = factor * seg
        idx = min(int(scaled), seg - 1)
        local = scaled - idx
        c1, c2 = colors[idx], colors[idx + 1]
        return (
            int(c1[0] + (c2[0] - c1[0]) * local),
            int(c1[1] + (c2[1] - c1[1]) * local),
            int(c1[2] + (c2[2] - c1[2]) * local),
        )

    @classmethod
    def title(cls, text: str) -> str:
        """渲染标题"""
        return cls.c(cls.BOLD + cls.WHITE, f"\n  {text}")

    @classmethod
    def subtitle(cls, text: str) -> str:
        """渲染副标题"""
        return cls.c(cls.DIM + cls.WHITE, f"  {text}")

    @classmethod
    def divider(cls, width: int = 55) -> str:
        """细分隔线"""
        return cls.c(cls.DIM, "  " + "\u2500" * width)

    @classmethod
    def heavy_divider(cls, width: int = 55) -> str:
        """粗分隔线"""
        return cls.c(cls.CYAN, "  " + "\u2550" * width)

    @classmethod
    def kv(cls, key: str, value: str, kw: int = 20) -> str:
        """键值对"""
        return f"    {cls.c(cls.CYAN, key.ljust(kw))}{value}"

    @classmethod
    def status_dot(cls, status: str) -> str:
        """服务状态标记"""
        if status in ("active", "running"):
            return cls.c(cls.GREEN, f"\u25cf {status}")
        if status in ("inactive", "dead", "failed"):
            return cls.c(cls.RED, f"\u25cf {status}")
        return cls.c(cls.YELLOW, f"\u25cf {status}")

    @classmethod
    def enabled_mark(cls, enabled: str) -> str:
        """开机自启标记"""
        if enabled == "enabled":
            return cls.c(cls.GREEN, "\u2713 enabled")
        return cls.c(cls.RED, "\u2717 " + enabled)

    @classmethod
    def ok_fail(cls, value: Optional[str]) -> str:
        """OK/FAIL 标记"""
        if value:
            return cls.c(cls.GREEN, "OK") + f"  {value}"
        return cls.c(cls.RED, "FAIL")


# =============================================================================
# 日志
# =============================================================================


class ColoredFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    """带颜色的日志格式化器"""

    COLORS: Dict[str, str] = {
        "DEBUG": "\033[36m",
        "INFO": "\033[32m",
        "WARNING": "\033[33m",
        "ERROR": "\033[31m",
        "CRITICAL": "\033[35m",
    }
    RESET = "\033[0m"

    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        """格式化日志条目"""
        color = self.COLORS.get(record.levelname, self.RESET)
        record.levelname = f"{color}{record.levelname}{self.RESET}"
        return super().format(record)


def setup_logging(verbose: bool = False, log_file: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
    """配置全局日志"""
    level = logging.DEBUG if verbose else logging.INFO
    handlers: List[logging.Handler] = []

    ch = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    ch.setFormatter(
        ColoredFormatter("%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s", datefmt="%H:%M:%S")
    )
    handlers.append(ch)

    if log_file:
        log_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        fh = logging.FileHandler(log_file, encoding="utf-8")
        fh.setFormatter(
            logging.Formatter(
                "%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s", datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"
            )
        )
        handlers.append(fh)

    logging.basicConfig(level=level, handlers=handlers, force=True)


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# =============================================================================
# 数据模型
# =============================================================================


class OSType(Enum):
    """操作系统类型"""

    DEBIAN = "debian"
    UBUNTU = "ubuntu"
    CENTOS = "centos"
    RHEL = "rhel"
    ROCKY = "rocky"
    ALMALINUX = "almalinux"
    FEDORA = "fedora"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


@dataclass
class OSInfo:
    """操作系统信息"""

    os_type: OSType
    version: str
    codename: str = ""

    @property
    def is_debian_based(self) -> bool:
        """是否为 Debian 系列"""
        return self.os_type in (OSType.DEBIAN, OSType.UBUNTU)

    @property
    def is_rhel_based(self) -> bool:
        """是否为 RHEL 系列"""
        return self.os_type in (
            OSType.CENTOS, OSType.RHEL, OSType.ROCKY,
            OSType.ALMALINUX, OSType.FEDORA,
        )


@dataclass
class InstanceConfig:
    """WARP 实例配置"""

    name: str
    namespace: str
    veth_host: str
    veth_ns: str
    host_ip: str
    ns_ip: str
    host_cidr: str
    ns_cidr: str
    http_proxy_port: int
    warp_plus_key: str = ""
    lib_dir: Path = field(default_factory=Path)
    run_dir: Path = field(default_factory=Path)

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.lib_dir or self.lib_dir == Path():
            self.lib_dir = LIB_DIR_BASE / f"warp-{self.name}"
        if not self.run_dir or self.run_dir == Path():
            self.run_dir = RUN_DIR_BASE / f"warp-{self.name}"

    @property
    def svc_id(self) -> str:
        """HAProxy 后端服务 ID"""
        return f"svc{self.name[-1]}"

    @property
    def http2socks_svc(self) -> str:
        """http2socks 服务名"""
        return f"http2socks-warp{self.name[-1]}"

    @property
    def warp_svc(self) -> str:
        """WARP 服务名"""
        return f"warp-{self.name}"

    @property
    def forward_svc(self) -> str:
        """转发服务名"""
        return f"warp-{self.name}-forward"


@dataclass
class ClusterConfig:
    """集群配置"""

    instances: List[InstanceConfig] = field(default_factory=list)
    public_port: int = PUBLIC_HTTP_PORT
    stats_port: int = HAPROXY_STATS_PORT
    stats_user: str = HAPROXY_STATS_USER
    stats_pass: str = HAPROXY_STATS_PASS
    rotate_interval_hours: int = ROTATE_INTERVAL_HOURS
    health_check_interval: int = HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL

    @classmethod
    def default(cls) -> ClusterConfig:
        """创建默认配置"""
        return cls(
            instances=[
                InstanceConfig(
                    name="ns2", namespace=NS2_NAME,
                    veth_host=VETH2_HOST, veth_ns=VETH2_NS,
                    host_ip=HOST2_IP, ns_ip=NS2_IP,
                    host_cidr=HOST2_CIDR, ns_cidr=NS2_CIDR,
                    http_proxy_port=HTTP_PROXY_PORT_2,
                ),
                InstanceConfig(
                    name="ns3", namespace=NS3_NAME,
                    veth_host=VETH3_HOST, veth_ns=VETH3_NS,
                    host_ip=HOST3_IP, ns_ip=NS3_IP,
                    host_cidr=HOST3_CIDR, ns_cidr=NS3_CIDR,
                    http_proxy_port=HTTP_PROXY_PORT_3,
                ),
            ]
        )

    def save(self, path: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
        """保存配置到文件"""
        target = path or CONFIG_FILE
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        data = {
            "public_port": self.public_port,
            "stats_port": self.stats_port,
            "stats_user": self.stats_user,
            "stats_pass": self.stats_pass,
            "rotate_interval_hours": self.rotate_interval_hours,
            "health_check_interval": self.health_check_interval,
            "instances": [
                {"name": i.name, "warp_plus_key": i.warp_plus_key}
                for i in self.instances
            ],
        }
        with open(target, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            json.dump(data, fh, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Optional[Path] = None) -> ClusterConfig:
        """从文件加载配置, 不存在则返回默认"""
        target = path or CONFIG_FILE
        cfg = cls.default()
        if not target.exists():
            return cfg
        try:
            with open(target, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                data = json.load(fh)
            cfg.public_port = data.get("public_port", cfg.public_port)
            cfg.stats_port = data.get("stats_port", cfg.stats_port)
            cfg.stats_user = data.get("stats_user", cfg.stats_user)
            cfg.stats_pass = data.get("stats_pass", cfg.stats_pass)
            cfg.rotate_interval_hours = data.get(
                "rotate_interval_hours", cfg.rotate_interval_hours
            )
            cfg.health_check_interval = data.get(
                "health_check_interval", cfg.health_check_interval
            )
            for idata in data.get("instances", []):
                for inst in cfg.instances:
                    if inst.name == idata.get("name"):
                        inst.warp_plus_key = idata.get("warp_plus_key", "")
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError) as exc:
            logger.warning(f"配置解析失败, 使用默认值: {exc}")
        return cfg


# =============================================================================
# 工具函数
# =============================================================================


def run_cmd(
    cmd: List[str],
    *,
    check: bool = True,
    capture: bool = True,
    timeout: int = 60,
    input_text: Optional[str] = None,
) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    """执行外部命令, check=False 时超时也不抛异常"""
    try:
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            check=check,
            capture_output=capture,
            text=True,
            timeout=timeout,
            input=input_text,
        )
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
        logger.debug(f"命令失败: {' '.join(cmd)} | stderr: {exc.stderr}")
        raise
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        logger.warning(f"命令超时 ({timeout}s): {' '.join(cmd)}")
        if check:
            raise
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, returncode=-1, stdout="", stderr="")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        logger.debug(f"命令不存在: {cmd[0]}")
        if check:
            raise
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, returncode=-1, stdout="", stderr="")


def run_ok(cmd: List[str], *, timeout: int = 60) -> bool:
    """执行命令并返回是否成功"""
    try:
        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
        return True
    except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired, FileNotFoundError):
        return False


def has_cmd(name: str) -> bool:
    """检查命令是否存在"""
    return shutil.which(name) is not None


def detect_os() -> OSInfo:
    """检测操作系统信息"""
    p = Path("/etc/os-release")
    if not p.exists():
        return OSInfo(OSType.UNKNOWN, "unknown")
    info: Dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in p.read_text().splitlines():
        if "=" in line:
            k, v = line.split("=", 1)
            info[k] = v.strip('"')
    m = {
        "debian": OSType.DEBIAN, "ubuntu": OSType.UBUNTU,
        "centos": OSType.CENTOS, "rhel": OSType.RHEL,
        "rocky": OSType.ROCKY, "almalinux": OSType.ALMALINUX,
        "fedora": OSType.FEDORA,
    }
    return OSInfo(
        os_type=m.get(info.get("ID", "").lower(), OSType.UNKNOWN),
        version=info.get("VERSION_ID", ""),
        codename=info.get("VERSION_CODENAME", ""),
    )

def check_warp_glibc_compat(os_info: OSInfo) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    """检查当前系统是否满足官方 cloudflare-warp 的 glibc 要求"""
    if os_info.os_type in (OSType.CENTOS, OSType.RHEL) and os_info.version.startswith("7"):
        return (
            False,
            "CentOS/RHEL 7 的 glibc 版本过低, 官方 cloudflare-warp/warp-cli 无法运行. "
            "请使用 Rocky/AlmaLinux 8+/9+, CentOS Stream, Debian 11+/12+, Ubuntu 20.04+/22.04+.",
        )
    return True, ""

def get_public_ip() -> str:
    """获取主机公网 IP"""
    for url in IP_CHECK_URLS:
        try:
            r = run_cmd(
                ["curl", "-s", "--max-time", "5", url],
                check=True, timeout=10,
            )
            if r.stdout.strip():
                return r.stdout.strip()
        except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            continue
    return "unknown"


def get_proxy_ip(port: int, host: str = "127.0.0.1") -> Optional[str]:
    """通过代理获取出口 IP"""
    for url in IP_CHECK_URLS:
        try:
            r = run_cmd(
                [
                    "curl", "-sk", "--max-time", str(CURL_TIMEOUT),
                    "-x", f"http://{host}:{port}", url,
                ],
                check=True, timeout=CURL_TIMEOUT + 5,
            )
            if r.stdout.strip():
                return r.stdout.strip()
        except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            continue
    return None


def proxy_healthy(port: int, host: str = "127.0.0.1") -> bool:
    """检查代理是否健康"""
    try:
        r = run_cmd(
            [
                "curl", "-sk", "--max-time", "15",
                "-x", f"http://{host}:{port}", IP_CHECK_URLS[0],
            ],
            check=True, timeout=20,
        )
        return bool(r.stdout.strip())
    except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return False


def kill_port(port: int) -> None:
    """杀死占用指定端口的进程"""
    run_cmd(["fuser", "-k", f"{port}/tcp"], check=False, timeout=10)


def kill_pattern(pat: str) -> None:
    """按模式杀死进程"""
    run_cmd(["pkill", "-f", pat], check=False, timeout=10)


def trim_log(path: Path, max_bytes: int = 10 * 1024 * 1024, keep: int = 1000) -> None:
    """裁剪日志文件"""
    if not path.exists():
        return
    try:
        if path.stat().st_size > max_bytes:
            lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines(
                keepends=True
            )
            path.write_text("".join(lines[-keep:]), encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError:
        pass


def write_file(path: Path, content: str, *, exe: bool = False) -> None:
    """写入文件, 可选设为可执行"""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
    if exe:
        path.chmod(0o755)


def ns_exists(name: str) -> bool:
    """检查网络命名空间是否存在"""
    r = run_cmd(["ip", "netns", "list"], check=False)
    return name in r.stdout


def iface_exists(name: str) -> bool:
    """检查网络接口是否存在"""
    return run_ok(["ip", "link", "show", name])


def svc_status(name: str) -> str:
    """获取 systemd 服务状态"""
    return run_cmd(["systemctl", "is-active", name], check=False).stdout.strip()


def svc_enabled(name: str) -> str:
    """获取 systemd 服务开机自启状态"""
    return run_cmd(["systemctl", "is-enabled", name], check=False).stdout.strip()


# =============================================================================
# 终端输入处理器
# =============================================================================


class TerminalInput:
    """
    终端输入处理器 - 支持 ghost text 自动补全和命令历史
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        commands: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        history_file: Optional[Path] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._commands: List[str] = sorted(commands) if commands else []
        self._history_file = history_file
        self._history: List[str] = []
        self._max_history = 500
        self._platform = self._detect_platform()
        self._load_history()

    def _detect_platform(self) -> str:
        """检测平台能力: 'windows' / 'unix' / 'fallback'"""
        try:
            if not hasattr(sys.stdin, "isatty") or not sys.stdin.isatty():
                return "fallback"
            if not hasattr(sys.stdout, "isatty") or not sys.stdout.isatty():
                return "fallback"
            if os.environ.get("TERM", "") == "dumb":
                return "fallback"
        except Exception:
            return "fallback"

        if sys.platform == "win32":
            try:
                import msvcrt  # noqa: F401
                # 启用 ANSI 转义支持
                self._enable_windows_ansi()
                return "windows"
            except Exception:
                return "fallback"
        else:
            try:
                import select  # noqa: F401
                import termios  # noqa: F401
                import tty  # noqa: F401
                sys.stdin.fileno()
                return "unix"
            except Exception:
                return "fallback"

    @staticmethod
    def _enable_windows_ansi() -> None:
        """启用 Windows 控制台 ANSI 转义序列支持"""
        try:
            import ctypes
            kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            # stdout handle
            handle = kernel32.GetStdHandle(-11)
            mode = ctypes.c_ulong()
            kernel32.GetConsoleMode(handle, ctypes.byref(mode))
            # ENABLE_VIRTUAL_TERMINAL_PROCESSING = 0x0004
            kernel32.SetConsoleMode(handle, mode.value | 0x0004)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def update_commands(self, commands: List[str]) -> None:
        """更新可补全的命令列表"""
        self._commands = sorted(commands)

    def _load_history(self) -> None:
        """从文件加载命令历史"""
        if not self._history_file or not self._history_file.exists():
            return
        try:
            text = self._history_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            lines = [line for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip()]
            self._history = lines[-self._max_history:]
        except OSError:
            pass

    def save_history(self) -> None:
        """保存命令历史到文件"""
        if not self._history_file:
            return
        try:
            self._history_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            content = "\n".join(self._history[-self._max_history:])
            self._history_file.write_text(content + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        except OSError:
            pass

    def add_history(self, line: str) -> None:
        """添加历史记录"""
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped:
            return
        if self._history and self._history[-1] == stripped:
            return
        self._history.append(stripped)
        if len(self._history) > self._max_history:
            self._history.pop(0)

    def _get_ghost(self, text: str) -> str:
        """获取 ghost text (实时显示第一个匹配的剩余部分)"""
        if not text or " " in text:
            return ""
        matches = [c for c in self._commands if c.startswith(text) and c != text]
        if matches:
            return matches[0][len(text):]
        return ""

    def _tab_complete(self, text: str) -> str:
        """获取 Tab 补全文本"""
        if not text or " " in text:
            return ""
        matches = [c for c in self._commands if c.startswith(text)]
        if not matches:
            return ""
        if len(matches) == 1:
            return matches[0][len(text):]
        prefix = matches[0]
        for m in matches[1:]:
            i = 0
            while i < len(prefix) and i < len(m) and prefix[i] == m[i]:
                i += 1
            prefix = prefix[:i]
        return prefix[len(text):]

    def readline(self, prompt: str = "> ") -> str:
        """读取一行输入"""
        if self._platform == "windows":
            try:
                return self._windows_readline(prompt)
            except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
                raise
            except Exception:
                return self._fallback_readline(prompt)
        elif self._platform == "unix":
            try:
                return self._unix_readline(prompt)
            except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
                raise
            except Exception:
                return self._fallback_readline(prompt)
        return self._fallback_readline(prompt)

    def _fallback_readline(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """回退到标准 input()"""
        return input(prompt)

    # ─── 通用编辑逻辑 ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _process_key(
        self,
        key: str,
        buf: List[str],
        cursor: int,
        hist_idx: int,
        saved_input: str,
    ) -> Tuple[Optional[str], List[str], int, int, str]:
        """
        处理单个按键, 返回 (result, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input)
        result 非 None 时表示输入完成
        """
        if key == "RETURN":
            return "".join(buf), buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "INTERRUPT":
            raise KeyboardInterrupt

        if key == "EOF":
            if not buf:
                raise EOFError
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "TAB":
            text = "".join(buf[:cursor])
            completion = self._tab_complete(text)
            if completion:
                for c in completion:
                    buf.insert(cursor, c)
                    cursor += 1
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "BACKSPACE":
            if cursor > 0:
                buf.pop(cursor - 1)
                cursor -= 1
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "DELETE":
            if cursor < len(buf):
                buf.pop(cursor)
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "LEFT":
            if cursor > 0:
                cursor -= 1
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "RIGHT":
            if cursor < len(buf):
                cursor += 1
            elif cursor == len(buf):
                ghost = self._get_ghost("".join(buf))
                if ghost:
                    buf.append(ghost[0])
                    cursor += 1
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "HOME":
            return None, buf, 0, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "END":
            return None, buf, len(buf), hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "UP":
            if self._history:
                if hist_idx == -1:
                    saved_input = "".join(buf)
                    hist_idx = len(self._history) - 1
                elif hist_idx > 0:
                    hist_idx -= 1
                else:
                    return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input
                buf = list(self._history[hist_idx])
                cursor = len(buf)
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "DOWN":
            if hist_idx >= 0:
                if hist_idx < len(self._history) - 1:
                    hist_idx += 1
                    buf = list(self._history[hist_idx])
                else:
                    hist_idx = -1
                    buf = list(saved_input)
                    saved_input = ""
                cursor = len(buf)
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "CTRL_U":
            return None, [], 0, -1, ""

        if key == "CTRL_K":
            return None, buf[:cursor], cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "CTRL_W":
            p = cursor
            while p > 0 and buf[p - 1] == " ":
                p -= 1
            while p > 0 and buf[p - 1] != " ":
                p -= 1
            del buf[p:cursor]
            return None, buf, p, hist_idx, saved_input

        if key == "ESCAPE":
            return None, [], 0, -1, ""

        # 可打印字符 (包括多字节 UTF-8)
        if len(key) >= 1 and ord(key[0]) >= 32:
            for c in key:
                buf.insert(cursor, c)
                cursor += 1
            return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

        return None, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input

    def _redraw(
        self, prompt: str, buf: List[str], cursor: int,
        ghost: str, prompt_width: int,
    ) -> None:
        """重绘整行: prompt + 语法高亮 buffer + ghost, 光标定位"""
        buffer_str = "".join(buf)
        colored_buf = self._syntax_highlight(buffer_str)

        out = f"\r\033[K{prompt}{colored_buf}"
        if ghost:
            out += f"\033[38;5;243m{ghost}\033[0m"
        target = prompt_width + cursor
        out += f"\r\033[{target}C" if target > 0 else "\r"
        _safe_write(out)

    def _syntax_highlight(self, text: str) -> str:
        """对输入文本进行语法高亮"""
        if not text:
            return ""

        parts = text.split(" ", 1)
        cmd = parts[0]
        args = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else ""

        # 命令部分着色
        if cmd in self._commands:
            # 有效命令: 绿色
            colored = f"\033[38;5;114m{cmd}\033[0m"
        elif any(c.startswith(cmd) for c in self._commands):
            # 部分匹配: 黄色
            colored = f"\033[38;5;222m{cmd}\033[0m"
        else:
            # 无效命令: 红色
            colored = f"\033[38;5;203m{cmd}\033[0m"

        if not args:
            return colored

        # 参数部分着色
        colored_args = self._highlight_args(args)
        return colored + " " + colored_args

    def _highlight_args(self, args: str) -> str:
        """对参数部分进行语法高亮"""
        result: List[str] = []
        i = 0
        while i < len(args):
            if args[i:].startswith("--"):
                # 长选项 --key1: 青色
                end = i + 2
                while end < len(args) and args[end] not in (" ", "="):
                    end += 1
                result.append(f"\033[38;5;81m{args[i:end]}\033[0m")
                i = end
            elif args[i] == "-" and i + 1 < len(args) and args[i + 1] != " ":
                # 短选项 -v: 青色
                end = i + 1
                while end < len(args) and args[end] not in (" ", "="):
                    end += 1
                result.append(f"\033[38;5;81m{args[i:end]}\033[0m")
                i = end
            elif args[i] == " ":
                result.append(" ")
                i += 1
            else:
                # 普通参数值: 白色
                end = i
                while end < len(args) and args[end] != " ":
                    end += 1
                result.append(f"\033[38;5;252m{args[i:end]}\033[0m")
                i = end
        return "".join(result)

    def _finish_line(self, prompt: str, buf: List[str]) -> None:
        """完成输入行"""
        _safe_write(f"\r\033[K{prompt}{''.join(buf)}\n")

    # ─── Windows 实现 ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _windows_readline(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """Windows 交互式输入"""
        import msvcrt

        buf: List[str] = []
        cursor = 0
        hist_idx = -1
        saved_input = ""
        prompt_width = len(_strip_ansi(prompt))

        self._redraw(prompt, buf, cursor, "", prompt_width)

        while True:
            # msvcrt.getwch() 阻塞读取一个宽字符
            ch = msvcrt.getwch()

            key = self._windows_translate_key(ch)

            result, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input = self._process_key(
                key, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input,
            )

            if result is not None:
                self._finish_line(prompt, list(result))
                return result

            ghost = self._get_ghost("".join(buf)) if cursor == len(buf) else ""
            self._redraw(prompt, buf, cursor, ghost, prompt_width)

    def _windows_translate_key(self, ch: str) -> str:
        """将 Windows 按键转换为标准键名"""
        import msvcrt

        if ch == "\r" or ch == "\n":
            return "RETURN"
        if ch == "\x03":
            return "INTERRUPT"
        if ch == "\x04" or ch == "\x1a":
            return "EOF"
        if ch == "\t":
            return "TAB"
        if ch == "\x08" or ch == "\x7f":
            return "BACKSPACE"
        if ch == "\x01":
            return "HOME"
        if ch == "\x05":
            return "END"
        if ch == "\x15":
            return "CTRL_U"
        if ch == "\x0b":
            return "CTRL_K"
        if ch == "\x17":
            return "CTRL_W"
        if ch == "\x1b":
            return "ESCAPE"

        # 特殊键前缀 (0x00 or 0xE0)
        if ch == "\x00" or ch == "\xe0":
            ch2 = msvcrt.getwch()
            win_map = {
                "H": "UP", "P": "DOWN",
                "K": "LEFT", "M": "RIGHT",
                "G": "HOME", "O": "END",
                "S": "DELETE",
            }
            return win_map.get(ch2, "")

        if ord(ch) >= 32:
            return ch

        return ""

    # ─── Unix 实现 ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _unix_readline(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """Unix 交互式输入"""
        import select
        import termios
        import tty

        fd = sys.stdin.fileno()
        old_settings = termios.tcgetattr(fd)

        buf: List[str] = []
        cursor = 0
        hist_idx = -1
        saved_input = ""
        prompt_width = len(_strip_ansi(prompt))

        try:
            tty.setcbreak(fd)
            self._redraw(prompt, buf, cursor, "", prompt_width)

            while True:
                readable, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [])
                if not readable:
                    continue

                raw = os.read(fd, 32)
                if not raw:
                    raise EOFError

                keys = self._unix_parse_input(fd, raw)

                for key in keys:
                    if not key:
                        continue
                    result, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input = self._process_key(
                        key, buf, cursor, hist_idx, saved_input,
                    )
                    if result is not None:
                        self._finish_line(prompt, list(result))
                        return result

                ghost = self._get_ghost("".join(buf)) if cursor == len(buf) else ""
                self._redraw(prompt, buf, cursor, ghost, prompt_width)

        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            raise
        finally:
            try:
                termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSADRAIN, old_settings)
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _unix_parse_input(self, fd: int, raw: bytes) -> List[str]:
        """将 Unix 原始字节解析为按键事件列表"""
        import select

        keys: List[str] = []
        i = 0

        while i < len(raw):
            b = raw[i]
            i += 1

            if b == 0x0D or b == 0x0A:
                keys.append("RETURN")
            elif b == 0x03:
                keys.append("INTERRUPT")
            elif b == 0x04:
                keys.append("EOF")
            elif b == 0x09:
                keys.append("TAB")
            elif b == 0x7F or b == 0x08:
                keys.append("BACKSPACE")
            elif b == 0x01:
                keys.append("HOME")
            elif b == 0x05:
                keys.append("END")
            elif b == 0x15:
                keys.append("CTRL_U")
            elif b == 0x0B:
                keys.append("CTRL_K")
            elif b == 0x17:
                keys.append("CTRL_W")
            elif b == 0x1B:
                if i < len(raw):
                    ch2 = raw[i]
                    i += 1
                    if ch2 == 0x5B:
                        if i < len(raw):
                            ch3 = raw[i]
                            i += 1
                            csi_map = {
                                0x41: "UP", 0x42: "DOWN",
                                0x43: "RIGHT", 0x44: "LEFT",
                                0x48: "HOME", 0x46: "END",
                            }
                            if ch3 in csi_map:
                                keys.append(csi_map[ch3])
                            elif 0x30 <= ch3 <= 0x39:
                                seq = chr(ch3)
                                while i < len(raw) and raw[i] != 0x7E:
                                    seq += chr(raw[i])
                                    i += 1
                                if i < len(raw) and raw[i] == 0x7E:
                                    i += 1
                                num = seq.split(";")[0]
                                num_map = {
                                    "1": "HOME", "3": "DELETE",
                                    "4": "END", "7": "HOME", "8": "END",
                                }
                                if num in num_map:
                                    keys.append(num_map[num])
                    elif ch2 == 0x4F:
                        if i < len(raw):
                            ch3 = raw[i]
                            i += 1
                            if ch3 == 0x48:
                                keys.append("HOME")
                            elif ch3 == 0x46:
                                keys.append("END")
                else:
                    readable, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.03)
                    if readable:
                        extra = os.read(fd, 32)
                        if extra:
                            keys.extend(self._unix_parse_input(fd, extra))
                    else:
                        keys.append("ESCAPE")
            elif b < 0x20:
                pass
            elif b < 0x80:
                keys.append(chr(b))
            else:
                need = 1 if b < 0xE0 else (2 if b < 0xF0 else 3)
                char_bytes = bytes([b])
                for _ in range(need):
                    if i < len(raw):
                        char_bytes += bytes([raw[i]])
                        i += 1
                    else:
                        try:
                            extra = os.read(fd, 1)
                            if extra:
                                char_bytes += extra
                        except OSError:
                            break
                try:
                    keys.append(char_bytes.decode("utf-8"))
                except UnicodeDecodeError:
                    pass

        return keys


# =============================================================================
# 网络命名空间
# =============================================================================


class NamespaceManager:
    """网络命名空间管理器"""

    def __init__(self, inst: InstanceConfig) -> None:
        self.inst = inst

    def create(self) -> None:
        """创建命名空间及 veth 对"""
        logger.info(f"创建命名空间 {self.inst.namespace}...")
        ns_etc = Path(f"/etc/netns/{self.inst.namespace}")
        ns_etc.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (ns_etc / "resolv.conf").write_text(
            "nameserver 1.1.1.1\nnameserver 8.8.8.8\n"
        )
        if not ns_exists(self.inst.namespace):
            run_cmd(["ip", "netns", "add", self.inst.namespace])
        if not iface_exists(self.inst.veth_host):
            run_cmd([
                "ip", "link", "add", self.inst.veth_host,
                "type", "veth", "peer", "name", self.inst.veth_ns,
            ])
            run_cmd([
                "ip", "link", "set", self.inst.veth_ns,
                "netns", self.inst.namespace,
            ])
            run_cmd(["ip", "addr", "add", self.inst.host_cidr, "dev", self.inst.veth_host])
            run_cmd(["ip", "link", "set", self.inst.veth_host, "up"])
            self._ns(["ip", "addr", "add", self.inst.ns_cidr, "dev", self.inst.veth_ns])
            self._ns(["ip", "link", "set", "lo", "up"])
            self._ns(["ip", "link", "set", self.inst.veth_ns, "up"])
            self._ns(["ip", "route", "add", "default", "via", self.inst.host_ip])
        logger.info(f"命名空间 {self.inst.namespace} 就绪")

    def delete(self) -> None:
        """删除命名空间"""
        run_cmd(["ip", "netns", "del", self.inst.namespace], check=False)
        run_cmd(["ip", "link", "del", self.inst.veth_host], check=False)
        p = Path(f"/etc/netns/{self.inst.namespace}")
        if p.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(p, ignore_errors=True)

    def setup_nat(self) -> None:
        """配置 NAT 转发"""
        run_cmd(["sysctl", "-w", "net.ipv4.ip_forward=1"], check=False)
        net = ".".join(self.inst.ns_ip.split(".")[:-1]) + ".0/24"
        if not run_ok([
            "iptables", "-t", "nat", "-C", "POSTROUTING",
            "-s", net, "-j", "MASQUERADE",
        ]):
            run_cmd([
                "iptables", "-t", "nat", "-A", "POSTROUTING",
                "-s", net, "-j", "MASQUERADE",
            ], check=False)
        for flag in ("-i", "-o"):
            if not run_ok([
                "iptables", "-C", "FORWARD",
                flag, self.inst.veth_host, "-j", "ACCEPT",
            ]):
                run_cmd([
                    "iptables", "-A", "FORWARD",
                    flag, self.inst.veth_host, "-j", "ACCEPT",
                ], check=False)

    def test(self) -> bool:
        """测试命名空间网络连通性"""
        try:
            self._ns(["ping", "-c", "1", "-W", "3", "1.1.1.1"])
            return True
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
            return False

    def _ns(
        self, cmd: List[str], *, check: bool = True
    ) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        """在命名空间中执行命令"""
        return run_cmd(
            ["ip", "netns", "exec", self.inst.namespace] + cmd, check=check
        )


# =============================================================================
# WARP 实例
# =============================================================================


class WarpInstance:
    """WARP 实例管理"""

    def __init__(self, inst: InstanceConfig) -> None:
        self.inst = inst

    def _cli(
        self,
        args: List[str],
        *,
        input_text: Optional[str] = None,
        timeout: int = 30,
    ) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        """在隔离 mount 和网络命名空间中执行 warp-cli"""
        cli_args = "--accept-tos " + " ".join(args)
        inner = (
            f"mkdir -p /var/lib/cloudflare-warp /run/cloudflare-warp && "
            f"mount --bind {self.inst.lib_dir} /var/lib/cloudflare-warp && "
            f"mount --bind {self.inst.run_dir} /run/cloudflare-warp && "
            f"exec ip netns exec {self.inst.namespace} /usr/bin/warp-cli {cli_args}"
        )
        return run_cmd(
            ["unshare", "-m", "--propagation", "private", "bash", "-c", inner],
            check=False, timeout=timeout, input_text=input_text,
        )

    def setup_dirs(self) -> None:
        """创建数据目录"""
        self.inst.lib_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.inst.run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def wipe_data(self) -> None:
        """清除并重建数据目录"""
        for d in [self.inst.lib_dir, self.inst.run_dir]:
            if d.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
            d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def register(self, delay: int = 0) -> bool:
        """注册 WARP 实例"""
        logger.info(f"注册 WARP ({self.inst.name})...")
        if delay > 0:
            logger.info(f"延迟 {delay} 秒后注册以尝试获取不同 IP...")
            time.sleep(delay)
        self._cli(["disconnect"])
        time.sleep(2)
        self._cli(["registration", "delete"])
        time.sleep(3)
        result = self._cli(["registration", "new"], input_text="y\n")
        if result.returncode != 0:
            logger.error(
                f"注册失败 ({self.inst.name}): "
                f"{result.stderr.strip() or result.stdout.strip()}"
            )
            return False
        logger.info(f"WARP 注册成功 ({self.inst.name})")
        time.sleep(2)
        if self.inst.warp_plus_key:
            logger.info(f"设置 WARP+ 密钥 ({self.inst.name})...")
            kr = self._cli(
                ["registration", "license", self.inst.warp_plus_key],
                input_text="y\n",
            )
            if kr.returncode != 0:
                logger.warning(f"WARP+ 密钥设置失败: {kr.stderr.strip()}")
            else:
                logger.info("WARP+ 密钥设置成功")
            time.sleep(2)
        return True

    def configure_proxy(self) -> bool:
        """配置 WARP 为代理模式"""
        logger.info(f"配置代理模式 ({self.inst.name})...")
        self._cli(["mode", "proxy"])
        time.sleep(1)
        self._cli(["proxy", "port", str(WARP_SOCKS_PORT)])
        time.sleep(1)
        return True

    def connect(self) -> bool:
        """连接 WARP"""
        logger.info(f"连接 WARP ({self.inst.name})...")
        self._cli(["connect"])
        time.sleep(8)
        st = self._cli(["status"])
        logger.info(f"WARP 状态 ({self.inst.name}): {st.stdout.strip()}")
        return True

    def full_cycle(self, delay: int = 0) -> bool:
        """完整注册->配置->连接流程"""
        if not self.register(delay=delay):
            return False
        self.configure_proxy()
        self.connect()
        return True

    def restart_svc_and_register(self, delay: int = 0) -> bool:
        """重启服务并重新注册以获取新 IP"""
        logger.info(f"重置 {self.inst.name} 以获取新 IP...")
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "stop", self.inst.warp_svc], check=False)
        time.sleep(2)
        self.wipe_data()
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "restart", self.inst.warp_svc])
        time.sleep(8)
        return self.full_cycle(delay=delay)


# =============================================================================
# HAProxy
# =============================================================================


class HAProxyManager:
    """HAProxy 管理器"""

    def __init__(self, cfg: ClusterConfig) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg

    def generate(self) -> str:
        """生成 HAProxy 配置"""
        srvs = []
        for i, inst in enumerate(self.cfg.instances):
            w = 200 if i == 0 else 100
            bk = "" if i == 0 else " backup"
            srvs.append(
                f"    server {inst.svc_id} 127.0.0.1:{inst.http_proxy_port} "
                f"check inter 5s fall 2 rise 1 weight {w}{bk}"
            )
        return textwrap.dedent(f"""\
            global
                log stdout format raw local0 info
                maxconn 8192
                stats socket {HAPROXY_SOCKET} mode 666 level admin expose-fd listeners
                stats timeout 2m
            defaults
                log     global
                mode    tcp
                option  tcplog
                option  dontlognull
                retries 3
                option  redispatch
                timeout connect 10s
                timeout client  120s
                timeout server  120s
                timeout queue   60s
            frontend http_proxy_in
                bind *:{self.cfg.public_port}
                default_backend warp_pool
            backend warp_pool
                balance first
        """) + "\n".join(srvs) + "\n\n" + textwrap.dedent(f"""\
            listen stats
                bind *:{self.cfg.stats_port}
                mode http
                stats enable
                stats uri /stats
                stats refresh 3s
                stats auth {self.cfg.stats_user}:{self.cfg.stats_pass}
                stats admin if TRUE
        """)

    def write(self) -> None:
        """写入 HAProxy 配置文件"""
        if HAPROXY_CONFIG.exists():
            shutil.copy(
                HAPROXY_CONFIG,
                HAPROXY_CONFIG.with_suffix(f".cfg.bak.{int(time.time())}"),
            )
        HAPROXY_CONFIG.write_text(self.generate())
        logger.info("HAProxy 配置已写入")

    def validate(self) -> bool:
        """验证 HAProxy 配置"""
        try:
            run_cmd(["haproxy", "-f", str(HAPROXY_CONFIG), "-c"])
            return True
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
            logger.error("HAProxy 配置验证失败")
            return False

    def restart(self) -> None:
        """重启 HAProxy"""
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "restart", "haproxy"])
        time.sleep(3)

    def send(self, cmd: str) -> str:
        """通过 UNIX socket 向 HAProxy 发送命令"""
        if not HAPROXY_SOCKET.exists():
            return ""
        try:
            r = run_cmd(
                ["bash", "-c", f'echo "{cmd}" | socat stdio {HAPROXY_SOCKET}'],
                check=False, timeout=5,
            )
            return r.stdout
        except (subprocess.CalledProcessError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            return ""

    def set_server(self, server: str, state: str) -> None:
        """设置后端服务器状态"""
        self.send(f"set server warp_pool/{server} state {state}")


# =============================================================================
# 依赖安装
# =============================================================================


class DepInstaller:
    """依赖安装器"""

    NEED = ("curl", "socat", "python3", "ip", "ss", "systemctl")

    def __init__(self, os_info: OSInfo) -> None:
        self.os = os_info

    def install(self) -> bool:
        """安装所有依赖"""
        logger.info("安装系统依赖...")
        ok, reason = check_warp_glibc_compat(self.os)
        if not ok:
            logger.error(reason)
            return False
        if self.os.is_debian_based:
            self._deb()
        elif self.os.is_rhel_based:
            self._rpm()
        else:
            logger.error(f"不支持的系统: {self.os.os_type.value}")
            return False
        if not has_cmd("warp-svc") or not has_cmd("warp-cli"):
            self._warp()
        for c in list(self.NEED) + ["warp-svc", "warp-cli", "haproxy"]:
            if not has_cmd(c):
                logger.error(f"缺少命令: {c}")
                return False
        logger.info("依赖安装完成")
        return True

    def _deb(self) -> None:
        """Debian/Ubuntu 依赖安装"""
        os.environ["DEBIAN_FRONTEND"] = "noninteractive"
        run_cmd(["apt-get", "update", "-qq"], check=False, timeout=120)
        run_cmd(
            [
                "apt-get", "install", "-y", "-qq",
                "curl", "wget", "socat", "haproxy", "cron",
                "python3", "iproute2", "net-tools", "gnupg2", "lsb-release",
                "apt-transport-https", "ca-certificates",
            ],
            check=False, timeout=600,
        )

    def _rpm(self) -> None:
        """RHEL 系列依赖安装"""
        run_cmd(
            ["yum", "install", "-y", "-q", "epel-release"],
            check=False, timeout=300,
        )
        run_cmd(
            [
                "yum", "install", "-y", "-q",
                "curl", "wget", "socat", "haproxy", "cronie",
                "python3", "iproute", "net-tools",
            ],
            check=False, timeout=600,
        )
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "enable", "crond"], check=False)
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "start", "crond"], check=False)

    def _warp(self) -> None:
        """安装 Cloudflare WARP 客户端"""
        logger.info("安装 Cloudflare WARP...")
        if self.os.is_debian_based:
            kr = Path("/usr/share/keyrings/cloudflare-warp-archive-keyring.gpg")
            kr.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            run_cmd(
                [
                    "bash", "-c",
                    f"curl -fsSL https://pkg.cloudflareclient.com/pubkey.gpg "
                    f"| gpg --yes --dearmor -o {kr}",
                ],
                check=False, timeout=120,
            )
            cn = self.os.codename or "bookworm"
            Path("/etc/apt/sources.list.d/cloudflare-client.list").write_text(
                f"deb [signed-by={kr}] https://pkg.cloudflareclient.com/ {cn} main\n"
            )
            run_cmd(["apt-get", "update", "-qq"], check=False, timeout=120)
            run_cmd(
                ["apt-get", "install", "-y", "-qq", "cloudflare-warp"],
                check=False, timeout=600,
            )
        else:
            Path("/etc/yum.repos.d/cloudflare-warp.repo").write_text(
                textwrap.dedent("""\
                    [cloudflare-warp]
                    name=cloudflare-warp
                    baseurl=https://pkg.cloudflareclient.com/rpm
                    enabled=1
                    gpgcheck=1
                    gpgkey=https://pkg.cloudflareclient.com/pubkey.gpg
                """)
            )
            run_cmd(["yum", "clean", "all", "-q"], check=False, timeout=120)
            run_cmd(["yum", "makecache", "-q"], check=False, timeout=300)
            run_cmd(
                ["yum", "install", "-y", "-q", "cloudflare-warp"],
                check=False, timeout=600,
            )


# =============================================================================
# 文件生成
# =============================================================================


class FileGen:
    """systemd 服务和脚本文件生成器"""

    SD = Path("/etc/systemd/system")

    @classmethod
    def create_all(cls, cfg: ClusterConfig) -> None:
        """创建所有文件"""
        STATE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        cls._boot_init(cfg)
        for inst in cfg.instances:
            cls._inst_files(inst)
        cls._http2socks()
        cls._unit("warp-monitor", cls._monitor_unit())
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "daemon-reload"])
        logger.info("脚本和服务创建完成")

    @classmethod
    def _boot_init(cls, cfg: ClusterConfig) -> None:
        """创建开机初始化脚本"""
        lines = [
            "#!/bin/bash",
            "set -e",
            "",
            "sysctl -w net.ipv4.ip_forward=1 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true",
            "",
        ]
        for inst in cfg.instances:
            net = ".".join(inst.ns_ip.split(".")[:-1]) + ".0/24"
            lines.extend([
                f"mkdir -p /etc/netns/{inst.namespace}",
                f"echo -e 'nameserver 1.1.1.1\\nnameserver 8.8.8.8' > /etc/netns/{inst.namespace}/resolv.conf",
                f"ip netns list | grep -qw {inst.namespace} || ip netns add {inst.namespace}",
                f"if ! ip link show {inst.veth_host} >/dev/null 2>&1; then",
                f"    ip link add {inst.veth_host} type veth peer name {inst.veth_ns}",
                f"    ip link set {inst.veth_ns} netns {inst.namespace}",
                f"    ip addr add {inst.host_cidr} dev {inst.veth_host}",
                f"    ip link set {inst.veth_host} up",
                f"    ip netns exec {inst.namespace} ip addr add {inst.ns_cidr} dev {inst.veth_ns}",
                f"    ip netns exec {inst.namespace} ip link set lo up",
                f"    ip netns exec {inst.namespace} ip link set {inst.veth_ns} up",
                f"    ip netns exec {inst.namespace} ip route add default via {inst.host_ip}",
                f"fi",
                f"iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -s {net} -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null || "
                f"iptables -t nat -A POSTROUTING -s {net} -j MASQUERADE",
                f"iptables -C FORWARD -i {inst.veth_host} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || "
                f"iptables -A FORWARD -i {inst.veth_host} -j ACCEPT",
                f"iptables -C FORWARD -o {inst.veth_host} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || "
                f"iptables -A FORWARD -o {inst.veth_host} -j ACCEPT",
                f"mkdir -p {inst.lib_dir} {inst.run_dir}",
                "",
            ])
        for port in [cfg.public_port, cfg.stats_port]:
            lines.append(
                f"iptables -C INPUT -p tcp --dport {port} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || "
                f"iptables -I INPUT -p tcp --dport {port} -j ACCEPT"
            )
        lines.append("\necho 'WARP cluster network init complete'")
        write_file(BIN_DIR / "warp-cluster-init.sh", "\n".join(lines) + "\n", exe=True)
        cls._unit(
            "warp-cluster-init",
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                [Unit]
                Description=WARP Cluster Network Initialization
                DefaultDependencies=no
                Before=warp-ns2.service warp-ns3.service haproxy.service
                After=network-pre.target systemd-sysctl.service
                Wants=network-pre.target
                [Service]
                Type=oneshot
                ExecStart={BIN_DIR}/warp-cluster-init.sh
                RemainAfterExit=yes
                [Install]
                WantedBy=multi-user.target
            """),
        )

    @classmethod
    def _inst_files(cls, inst: InstanceConfig) -> None:
        """创建单个实例的脚本和服务文件"""
        write_file(
            BIN_DIR / f"warp-{inst.name}-start.sh",
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                #!/bin/bash
                set -e
                mkdir -p {inst.lib_dir} {inst.run_dir}
                unshare -m --propagation private bash -c '
                  mkdir -p /var/lib/cloudflare-warp /run/cloudflare-warp
                  mount --bind {inst.lib_dir} /var/lib/cloudflare-warp
                  mount --bind {inst.run_dir} /run/cloudflare-warp
                  exec ip netns exec {inst.namespace} /usr/bin/warp-svc
                '
            """),
            exe=True,
        )
        write_file(
            BIN_DIR / f"warp-{inst.name}-cli.sh",
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                #!/bin/bash
                set -e
                unshare -m --propagation private bash -c '
                  mkdir -p /var/lib/cloudflare-warp /run/cloudflare-warp
                  mount --bind {inst.lib_dir} /var/lib/cloudflare-warp
                  mount --bind {inst.run_dir} /run/cloudflare-warp
                  exec ip netns exec {inst.namespace} /usr/bin/warp-cli --accept-tos "$@"
                ' bash "$@"
            """),
            exe=True,
        )
        write_file(
            BIN_DIR / f"warp-{inst.name}-forward.sh",
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                #!/bin/bash
                exec ip netns exec {inst.namespace} socat \\
                    TCP-LISTEN:{NS_FORWARD_PORT},bind={inst.ns_ip},fork,reuseaddr \\
                    TCP:127.0.0.1:{WARP_SOCKS_PORT}
            """),
            exe=True,
        )
        cls._unit(
            inst.warp_svc,
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                [Unit]
                Description=WARP in namespace {inst.namespace}
                After=network.target warp-cluster-init.service
                Requires=warp-cluster-init.service
                StartLimitIntervalSec=0
                [Service]
                Type=simple
                ExecStart={BIN_DIR}/warp-{inst.name}-start.sh
                Restart=always
                RestartSec=5
                StartLimitBurst=0
                [Install]
                WantedBy=multi-user.target
            """),
        )
        cls._unit(
            inst.forward_svc,
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                [Unit]
                Description=SOCKS5 forward for {inst.namespace}
                After={inst.warp_svc}.service
                Requires={inst.warp_svc}.service
                [Service]
                Type=simple
                ExecStart={BIN_DIR}/warp-{inst.name}-forward.sh
                Restart=always
                RestartSec=3
                [Install]
                WantedBy=multi-user.target
            """),
        )
        cls._unit(
            inst.http2socks_svc,
            textwrap.dedent(f"""\
                [Unit]
                Description=HTTP proxy for {inst.namespace}
                After={inst.forward_svc}.service
                Requires={inst.forward_svc}.service
                [Service]
                Type=simple
                ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 {BIN_DIR}/warp-http2socks.py {inst.ns_ip} {NS_FORWARD_PORT} {inst.http_proxy_port}
                Restart=always
                RestartSec=3
                [Install]
                WantedBy=multi-user.target
            """),
        )

    @classmethod
    def _http2socks(cls) -> None:
        """创建 HTTP 到 SOCKS5 桥接脚本"""
        script = (
            '#!/usr/bin/env python3\n'
            '"""HTTP to SOCKS5 proxy bridge"""\n'
            'import socket,threading,struct,sys,select\n'
            'SOCKS_HOST=sys.argv[1]\n'
            'SOCKS_PORT=int(sys.argv[2])\n'
            'LISTEN_PORT=int(sys.argv[3])\n\n'
            'def socks5_connect(h,p):\n'
            ' try:\n'
            '  s=socket.socket(socket.AF_INET,socket.SOCK_STREAM);s.settimeout(20);s.connect((SOCKS_HOST,SOCKS_PORT))\n'
            '  s.sendall(b"\\x05\\x01\\x00");r=s.recv(2)\n'
            '  if r!=b"\\x05\\x00":s.close();return None\n'
            '  hb=h.encode();s.sendall(b"\\x05\\x01\\x00\\x03"+bytes([len(hb)])+hb+struct.pack(">H",p));r=s.recv(10)\n'
            '  if len(r)<2 or r[1]!=0:s.close();return None\n'
            '  s.settimeout(None);return s\n'
            ' except:return None\n\n'
            'def relay(a,b):\n'
            ' try:\n'
            '  while 1:\n'
            '   r,_,_=select.select([a,b],[],[],90)\n'
            '   if not r:break\n'
            '   for s in r:\n'
            '    d=s.recv(8192)\n'
            '    if not d:return\n'
            '    (b if s is a else a).sendall(d)\n'
            ' except:pass\n'
            ' finally:\n'
            '  for s in(a,b):\n'
            '   try:s.close()\n'
            '   except:pass\n\n'
            'def handle(c):\n'
            ' try:\n'
            '  d=b""\n'
            '  while b"\\r\\n\\r\\n" not in d:\n'
            '   ch=c.recv(4096)\n'
            '   if not ch:c.close();return\n'
            '   d+=ch\n'
            '   if len(d)>131072:c.close();return\n'
            '  fl=d.split(b"\\r\\n",1)[0].decode(errors="ignore");ps=fl.split()\n'
            '  if len(ps)<3:c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 400 Bad Request\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n");c.close();return\n'
            '  m,t=ps[0],ps[1]\n'
            '  if m.upper()=="CONNECT":\n'
            '   if":"not in t:c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 400 Bad Request\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n");c.close();return\n'
            '   h,p=t.rsplit(":",1);p=int(p);r=socks5_connect(h,p)\n'
            '   if not r:c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 502 Bad Gateway\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n");c.close();return\n'
            '   c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\\r\\n\\r\\n");relay(c,r);return\n'
            '  if t.startswith("http://"):\n'
            '   u=t[7:];sl=u.find("/")\n'
            '   hp,pa=(u,"/") if sl==-1 else (u[:sl],u[sl:])\n'
            '   if":"in hp:h,p=hp.rsplit(":",1);p=int(p)\n'
            '   else:h=hp;p=80\n'
            '   r=socks5_connect(h,p)\n'
            '   if not r:c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 502 Bad Gateway\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n");c.close();return\n'
            '   nf=f"{m} {pa} HTTP/1.1\\r\\n".encode();rest=d.split(b"\\r\\n",1)[1];r.sendall(nf+rest);relay(c,r);return\n'
            '  c.sendall(b"HTTP/1.1 400 Bad Request\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n");c.close()\n'
            ' except:\n'
            '  try:c.close()\n'
            '  except:pass\n\n'
            'def main():\n'
            ' srv=socket.socket(socket.AF_INET,socket.SOCK_STREAM);srv.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET,socket.SO_REUSEADDR,1)\n'
            ' srv.bind(("127.0.0.1",LISTEN_PORT));srv.listen(256)\n'
            ' print(f"http2socks on 127.0.0.1:{LISTEN_PORT} -> socks5://{SOCKS_HOST}:{SOCKS_PORT}",flush=True)\n'
            ' while 1:\n'
            '  c,_=srv.accept();threading.Thread(target=handle,args=(c,),daemon=True).start()\n\n'
            'if __name__=="__main__":main()\n'
        )
        write_file(BIN_DIR / "warp-http2socks.py", script, exe=True)

    @classmethod
    def _monitor_unit(cls) -> str:
        """生成监控服务 unit 内容"""
        return textwrap.dedent(f"""\
            [Unit]
            Description=WARP Cluster Monitor
            After=network.target haproxy.service
            StartLimitIntervalSec=0
            [Service]
            Type=simple
            ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 {BIN_DIR}/warp-cluster.py monitor
            Restart=always
            RestartSec=10
            StartLimitBurst=0
            [Install]
            WantedBy=multi-user.target
        """)

    @classmethod
    def _unit(cls, name: str, content: str) -> None:
        """写入 systemd unit 文件"""
        (cls.SD / f"{name}.service").write_text(content)


# =============================================================================
# 清理器
# =============================================================================


class Cleaner:
    """完全清理器"""

    SVCS = [
        "warp-monitor", "warp-cluster-monitor", "warp-cluster-init",
        "warp-ns2", "warp-ns3", "warp-ns2-forward", "warp-ns3-forward",
        "http2socks-warp2", "http2socks-warp3",
        "wireproxy2", "wireproxy3", "warp-svc", "warp-svc2", "warp-svc3",
    ]
    UNITS = [
        "warp-ns2.service", "warp-ns3.service",
        "warp-ns2-forward.service", "warp-ns3-forward.service",
        "http2socks-warp2.service", "http2socks-warp3.service",
        "warp-monitor.service", "warp-cluster-monitor.service",
        "warp-cluster-init.service",
    ]
    SCRIPTS = [
        "warp-ns2-start.sh", "warp-ns3-start.sh",
        "warp-ns2-cli.sh", "warp-ns3-cli.sh",
        "warp-ns2-forward.sh", "warp-ns3-forward.sh",
        "warp-http2socks.py", "http2socks.py",
        "warp-cluster-manager.sh", "warp-cluster.py", "warp-cluster-init.sh",
        "warp", "warp-help", "warp-status", "warp-rotate", "warp-test",
    ]
    KILL = [
        "http2socks", "warp-http2socks",
        "warp-ns2-forward", "warp-ns3-forward",
        "warp-svc", "wireproxy", "socat.*41000",
    ]

    def run(self, full: bool = True) -> None:
        """执行清理"""
        logger.info("清理旧残留...")
        for s in self.SVCS:
            run_cmd(["systemctl", "stop", s], check=False)
            run_cmd(["systemctl", "disable", s], check=False)
        sd = Path("/etc/systemd/system")
        for n in self.UNITS:
            p = sd / n
            if p.exists():
                p.unlink()
        for n in self.SCRIPTS:
            p = BIN_DIR / n
            if p.exists():
                p.unlink()
        cr = Path("/etc/cron.d/warp-rotate")
        if cr.exists():
            cr.unlink()
        for pat in self.KILL:
            kill_pattern(pat)
        for port in [HTTP_PROXY_PORT_2, HTTP_PROXY_PORT_3, PUBLIC_HTTP_PORT, HAPROXY_STATS_PORT]:
            kill_port(port)
        for ns in [NS2_NAME, NS3_NAME]:
            run_cmd(["ip", "netns", "del", ns], check=False)
        for v in [VETH2_HOST, VETH3_HOST]:
            run_cmd(["ip", "link", "del", v], check=False)
        for ns in [NS2_NAME, NS3_NAME]:
            p = Path(f"/etc/netns/{ns}")
            if p.exists():
                shutil.rmtree(p, ignore_errors=True)
        if full:
            for d in [
                RUN_DIR_BASE / "warp-ns2", RUN_DIR_BASE / "warp-ns3",
                LIB_DIR_BASE / "warp-ns2", LIB_DIR_BASE / "warp-ns3",
            ]:
                if d.exists():
                    shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
            # 保留 STATE_DIR 本身 (里面有 history), 只删配置
            if CONFIG_FILE.exists():
                CONFIG_FILE.unlink(missing_ok=True)
            if ACTIVE_FILE.exists():
                ACTIVE_FILE.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        run_cmd(["systemctl", "daemon-reload"], check=False)
        logger.info("清理完成")


# =============================================================================
# 交互式命令行 REPL
# =============================================================================


class InteractiveShell:
    """交互式命令行界面 (带 ghost text 自动补全)"""

    COMMANDS: Dict[str, str] = {
        "status": "查看集群状态",
        "test": "测试代理连通性",
        "rotate": "手动轮换 IP",
        "deploy": "完整部署集群",
        "cleanup": "完全清理集群",
        "monitor": "启动监控守护进程",
        "help": "显示帮助信息",
        "clear": "清屏",
        "exit": "退出交互式命令行",
        "quit": "退出交互式命令行",
    }

    def __init__(self, manager: ClusterManager) -> None:
        self.mgr = manager
        self._terminal = TerminalInput(
            commands=list(self.COMMANDS.keys()),
            history_file=HISTORY_FILE,
        )
        atexit.register(self._safe_exit_cleanup)

    def _safe_exit_cleanup(self) -> None:
        """atexit 回调: 安全保存历史 (不抛任何异常)"""
        try:
            self._terminal.save_history()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _prompt(self) -> str:
        """生成提示符"""
        u = UI
        if u._color_ok():
            return f"{u.BOLD}{u.CYAN}WARP{u.RESET} {u.DIM}>{u.RESET} "
        return "WARP > "

    def _show_welcome(self) -> None:
        """显示欢迎界面"""
        u = UI
        print()
        print(u.banner())
        print(u.subtitle(f"双实例高可用 HTTP 代理集群 v{VERSION}"))
        print(u.heavy_divider())

        if CONFIG_FILE.exists():
            entry_ip = get_proxy_ip(self.mgr.cfg.public_port)
            ha_status = svc_status("haproxy")
            mon_status = svc_status("warp-monitor")

            print(u.title("集群概览"))
            print(u.kv("HAProxy", u.status_dot(ha_status)))
            print(u.kv("监控服务", u.status_dot(mon_status)))
            print(u.kv(
                "入口 IP",
                u.c(u.GREEN if entry_ip else u.RED, entry_ip or "未就绪"),
            ))
            print(u.kv("代理地址", f"http://{get_public_ip()}:{self.mgr.cfg.public_port}"))
            deployed = True
        else:
            print(u.title("未检测到部署"))
            print(f"    输入 {u.c(u.CYAN + u.BOLD, 'deploy')} 开始部署集群")
            deployed = False

        print(u.title("可用命令"))
        for cmd, desc in self.COMMANDS.items():
            if cmd == "quit":
                continue
            if not deployed and cmd in ("status", "test", "rotate", "monitor"):
                print(f"    {u.c(u.DIM, cmd.ljust(12))}{u.c(u.DIM, desc + ' (需先部署)')}")
            else:
                print(f"    {u.c(u.CYAN + u.BOLD, cmd.ljust(12))}{u.c(u.DIM, desc)}")

        print()
        print(u.subtitle("输入命令后按回车执行, Tab 补全, 方向键浏览历史"))
        print(u.heavy_divider())
        print()

    def _show_help(self) -> None:
        """显示帮助"""
        u = UI
        print(u.title("可用命令"))
        for cmd, desc in self.COMMANDS.items():
            if cmd == "quit":
                continue
            print(f"    {u.c(u.CYAN + u.BOLD, cmd.ljust(12))}{desc}")

        print(u.title("命令行模式"))
        print(f"    {u.c(u.DIM, 'warp status')}         直接执行子命令")
        print(f"    {u.c(u.DIM, 'warp deploy --key1 xxx')}  带参数执行")
        print()

    def _execute(self, line: str) -> bool:
        """
        执行单条命令

        Returns:
            False 表示应该退出 REPL
        """
        u = UI
        parts = line.strip().split()
        if not parts:
            return True

        cmd = parts[0].lower()

        if cmd in ("exit", "quit", "q"):
            print(u.c(u.DIM, "\n  再见!\n"))
            return False

        if cmd == "help":
            self._show_help()
            return True

        if cmd == "clear":
            _safe_write("\033[2J\033[H")
            return True

        if cmd == "status":
            self.mgr.status()
            return True

        if cmd == "test":
            self.mgr.test()
            return True

        if cmd == "rotate":
            self.mgr.rotate()
            return True

        if cmd == "deploy":
            keys: List[str] = []
            try:
                i = 1
                while i < len(parts):
                    if parts[i] == "--key1" and i + 1 < len(parts):
                        keys.append(parts[i + 1])
                        i += 2
                    elif parts[i] == "--key2" and i + 1 < len(parts):
                        if len(keys) == 0:
                            keys.append("")
                        keys.append(parts[i + 1])
                        i += 2
                    else:
                        i += 1
            except IndexError:
                pass
            self.mgr.deploy(keys=keys if keys else None)
            return True

        if cmd == "cleanup":
            try:
                confirm = input(
                    f"    {u.c(u.YELLOW, '确认清理所有 WARP 集群组件? (yes/no): ')}"
                )
                if confirm.strip().lower() in ("yes", "y"):
                    self.mgr.cleanup()
                else:
                    print(u.c(u.DIM, "    已取消"))
            except (EOFError, KeyboardInterrupt):
                print(u.c(u.DIM, "\n    已取消"))
            return True

        if cmd == "monitor":
            print(u.c(u.YELLOW, "    监控将在前台运行, 按 Ctrl+C 停止"))
            try:
                self.mgr.monitor()
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print(u.c(u.DIM, "\n    监控已停止"))
            return True

        print(f"    {u.c(u.RED, '未知命令:')} {cmd}")
        print(f"    输入 {u.c(u.CYAN, 'help')} 查看可用命令")
        return True

    def run(self) -> None:
        """启动交互式 REPL"""
        self._show_welcome()

        while True:
            try:
                line = self._terminal.readline(self._prompt())
                self._terminal.add_history(line)
                if not self._execute(line):
                    break
            except EOFError:
                print(UI.c(UI.DIM, "\n\n  再见!\n"))
                break
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print()
                continue
            except Exception as exc:
                print(UI.c(UI.RED, f"    错误: {exc}"))


# =============================================================================
# 集群管理器
# =============================================================================


class ClusterManager:
    """核心控制器"""

    def __init__(self, cfg: Optional[ClusterConfig] = None) -> None:
        self.cfg = cfg or ClusterConfig.load()
        self.os_info = detect_os()
        self.cleaner = Cleaner()
        self.haproxy = HAProxyManager(self.cfg)

    # =========================================================================
    # interactive
    # =========================================================================

    def interactive(self) -> None:
        """启动交互式命令行"""
        shell = InteractiveShell(self)
        shell.run()

    # =========================================================================
    # status
    # =========================================================================

    def status(self) -> None:
        """显示集群状态"""
        u = UI
        print()
        print(u.banner())
        print(u.subtitle(f"集群状态 | v{VERSION}"))
        print(u.heavy_divider())

        print(u.title("服务状态"))
        svc_rows: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = [
            ("HAProxy", f"0.0.0.0:{self.cfg.public_port}", "haproxy"),
            ("开机初始化", "-", "warp-cluster-init"),
            ("监控服务", "-", "warp-monitor"),
        ]
        for inst in self.cfg.instances:
            svc_rows.append(
                (f"WARP {inst.name}", f"127.0.0.1:{inst.http_proxy_port}", inst.http2socks_svc)
            )
        for label, addr, svc in svc_rows:
            print(f"    {label:<20} {addr:<22}{u.status_dot(svc_status(svc))}")

        active = ACTIVE_FILE.read_text().strip() if ACTIVE_FILE.exists() else "svc2"
        print(f"\n    当前活跃: {u.c(u.BOLD + u.GREEN, active)}")

        print(u.title("出口 IP"))
        for inst in self.cfg.instances:
            ip = get_proxy_ip(inst.http_proxy_port) or "未就绪"
            print(u.kv(inst.name, u.c(u.GREEN if ip != "未就绪" else u.RED, ip)))
        entry = get_proxy_ip(self.cfg.public_port) or "未就绪"
        print(u.kv(
            "入口",
            u.c(u.BOLD + (u.GREEN if entry != "未就绪" else u.RED), entry),
        ))

        print(u.title("监听端口"))
        ss = run_cmd(["ss", "-tlnp"], check=False).stdout
        watch = [self.cfg.public_port, self.cfg.stats_port] + [
            i.http_proxy_port for i in self.cfg.instances
        ]
        for line in ss.splitlines():
            for p in watch:
                if f":{p} " in line or f":{p}\t" in line:
                    print(f"    {u.c(u.DIM, line.strip())}")
                    break

        print(u.title("开机自启"))
        svcs = ["haproxy", "warp-cluster-init", "warp-monitor"]
        for inst in self.cfg.instances:
            svcs.extend([inst.warp_svc, inst.forward_svc, inst.http2socks_svc])
        for s in svcs:
            print(f"    {s:<28}{u.enabled_mark(svc_enabled(s))}")

        if ROTATE_LOG.exists():
            print(u.title("最近轮换"))
            for line in ROTATE_LOG.read_text(errors="ignore").splitlines()[-6:]:
                print(f"    {u.c(u.DIM, line)}")

        print(u.heavy_divider())
        print()

    # =========================================================================
    # test
    # =========================================================================

    def test(self) -> None:
        """测试代理连通性"""
        u = UI
        print(u.title("代理连通性测试"))
        print(u.divider())
        for inst in self.cfg.instances:
            print(u.kv(
                f"端口 {inst.http_proxy_port}",
                u.ok_fail(get_proxy_ip(inst.http_proxy_port)),
            ))
        print(u.kv(
            f"入口 {self.cfg.public_port}",
            u.c(u.BOLD, "") + u.ok_fail(get_proxy_ip(self.cfg.public_port)),
        ))
        print(u.divider())
        print()

    # =========================================================================
    # deploy
    # =========================================================================

    def deploy(self, keys: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> bool:
        """完整部署"""
        u = UI
        print()
        print(u.banner())
        print(u.subtitle(f"v{VERSION} | 外网入口: 0.0.0.0:{self.cfg.public_port}"))
        print(u.heavy_divider())

        if os.geteuid() != 0:
            logger.error("请使用 root 权限运行")
            return False

        logger.info(f"系统: {self.os_info.os_type.value} {self.os_info.version}")
        ok, reason = check_warp_glibc_compat(self.os_info)
        if not ok:
            logger.error(reason)
            logger.error("部署中止: 当前系统不支持官方 WARP 客户端")
            return False
        if keys:
            for i, k in enumerate(keys):
                if i < len(self.cfg.instances) and k:
                    self.cfg.instances[i].warp_plus_key = k

        steps: List[Tuple[str, Callable[[], bool]]] = [
            ("清理旧残留", self._s1),
            ("安装依赖", self._s2),
            ("创建命名空间网络", self._s3),
            ("创建脚本和服务", self._s4),
            ("启动 WARP 实例", self._s5),
            ("配置 HAProxy", self._s6),
            ("创建管理脚本", self._s7),
            ("配置定时任务和监控", self._s8),
            ("配置防火墙", self._s9),
            ("验证部署", self._s10),
        ]

        for idx, (name, fn) in enumerate(steps, 1):
            logger.info("")
            logger.info(f"[{idx}/{len(steps)}] {name}...")
            try:
                if not fn():
                    logger.error(f"步骤失败: {name}")
                    return False
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error(f"步骤异常: {name} - {exc}")
                return False

        self.cfg.save()
        self._result()
