#### TDD Best Practices

##### Unit Test Examples

```python
# tests/unit/test_user.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from src.domain.entities.user import User
from src.domain.value_objects.money import Money


class TestUser:
    """用户实体单元测试。"""

    def test_user_creation_with_auto_id(self) -> None:
        """测试用户创建时自动生成ID。"""
        user = User(name="张三", email="zhangsan@example.com")
        assert user.name == "张三"
        assert user.email == "zhangsan@example.com"
        assert user.id is not None

    def test_two_users_have_different_ids(self) -> None:
        """测试两个用户实例拥有不同的ID。"""
        user1 = User(name="张三", email="zhangsan@example.com")
        user2 = User(name="李四", email="lisi@example.com")
        assert user1.id != user2.id

    def test_change_email_success(self) -> None:
        """测试成功更改邮箱。"""
        user = User(name="张三", email="zhangsan@example.com")
        user.change_email("new@example.com")
        assert user.email == "new@example.com"

    def test_change_email_invalid_format(self) -> None:
        """测试无效邮箱格式抛出异常。"""
        user = User(name="张三", email="zhangsan@example.com")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Invalid email format"):
            user.change_email("invalid-email")

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "invalid_email",
        [
            "not-an-email",
            "missing@tld",
            "@no-local.com",
            "",
            "spaces in@email.com",
        ],
    )
    def test_change_email_various_invalid_formats(self, invalid_email: str) -> None:
        """参数化测试各种无效邮箱格式。"""
        user = User(name="张三", email="zhangsan@example.com")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            user.change_email(invalid_email)


class TestMoney:
    """货币值对象单元测试。"""

    def test_money_creation(self) -> None:
        """测试货币对象创建。"""
        money = Money(100.0, "USD")
        assert money.amount == 100.0
        assert money.currency == "USD"

    def test_money_default_currency_is_usd(self) -> None:
        """测试默认货币为USD。"""
        money = Money(50.0)
        assert money.currency == "USD"

    def test_money_add_same_currency(self) -> None:
        """测试相同货币加法返回新对象。"""
        money1 = Money(100.0, "USD")
        money2 = Money(50.0, "USD")
        result = money1.add(money2)
        assert result.amount == 150.0
        assert result.currency == "USD"
        # 验证不可变性：原对象未被修改
        assert money1.amount == 100.0

    def test_money_add_different_currency_raises_error(self) -> None:
        """测试不同货币加法抛出异常。"""
        money1 = Money(100.0, "USD")
        money2 = Money(50.0, "EUR")
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Cannot add different currencies"):
            money1.add(money2)

    def test_money_negative_amount_raises_error(self) -> None:
        """测试负数金额抛出异常。"""
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Amount cannot be negative"):
            Money(-1.0, "USD")

    def test_money_immutability(self) -> None:
        """测试货币对象不可变性（frozen dataclass）。"""
        money = Money(100.0, "USD")
        with pytest.raises((AttributeError, TypeError)):
            money.amount = 200.0  # type: ignore[misc]

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "amount, currency",
        [
            (0.0, "USD"),
            (0.01, "CNY"),
            (9999999.99, "EUR"),
        ],
    )
    def test_money_creation_valid_amounts(self, amount: float, currency: str) -> None:
        """参数化测试有效金额创建货币对象。"""
        money = Money(amount, currency)
        assert money.amount == amount
        assert money.currency == currency
```

##### Integration Test Examples

```python
# tests/integration/test_create_user.py
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from src.application.use_cases.create_user import CreateUserUseCase
from src.infrastructure.repositories.sql_user_repository import SQLUserRepository


@pytest.mark.asyncio
@pytest.mark.integration
async def test_create_user_use_case(db_session: object) -> None:
    """测试创建用户用例的完整集成流程。"""
    repository = SQLUserRepository(db_session)
    use_case = CreateUserUseCase(repository)

    user_id = await use_case.execute("张三", "zhangsan@example.com")

    created_user = await repository.find_by_id(user_id)
    assert created_user is not None
    assert created_user.name == "张三"
    assert created_user.email == "zhangsan@example.com"
```

#### Async Programming Standards

```python
# src/infrastructure/adapters/user_api_adapter.py
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from typing import List

import aiohttp


async def fetch_user_data(
    session: aiohttp.ClientSession,
    user_id: int,
    base_url: str,
) -> dict:
    """异步获取单个用户数据。

    Args:
        session: 复用的 aiohttp 客户端会话
        user_id: 用户ID
        base_url: API 基础URL

    Returns:
        dict: 用户数据字典

    Raises:
        aiohttp.ClientResponseError: 当HTTP响应状态码非2xx时抛出
    """
    async with session.get(f"{base_url}/api/users/{user_id}") as response:
        response.raise_for_status()
        return await response.json()


async def process_users(user_ids: List[int], base_url: str) -> List[dict]:
    """并发处理多个用户数据获取。

    通过复用单个 ClientSession 提升性能，避免每次请求新建会话。

    Args:
        user_ids: 用户ID列表
        base_url: API 基础URL

    Returns:
        List[dict]: 用户数据列表
    """
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
        tasks = [
            fetch_user_data(session, user_id, base_url)
            for user_id in user_ids
        ]
        return await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
```

#### Error Handling and Logging

##### Complete Logging Configuration

```python
# src/infrastructure/logging_config.py
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import logging.handlers
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def configure_logging(
    log_level: str = "INFO",
    log_file: Path | None = None,
) -> None:
    """配置全局日志系统。

    Args:
        log_level: 日志级别，默认为INFO
        log_file: 日志文件路径，为None时仅输出到控制台
    """
    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
    root_logger.setLevel(getattr(logging, log_level.upper(), logging.INFO))

    # 统一的日志格式
    formatter = logging.Formatter(
        fmt="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s:%(funcName)s:%(lineno)d | %(message)s",
        datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
    )

    # 控制台处理器
    console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    root_logger.addHandler(console_handler)

    # 文件处理器（可选）
    if log_file is not None:
        log_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        file_handler = logging.handlers.RotatingFileHandler(
            filename=log_file,
            maxBytes=10 * 1024 * 1024,  # 10MB
            backupCount=5,
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        root_logger.addHandler(file_handler)
```

##### Exception Hierarchy and Service Example

```python
# src/domain/exceptions.py
from __future__ import annotations


class DomainError(Exception):
    """领域层异常基类。"""


class UserServiceError(DomainError):
    """用户服务异常基类。"""


class UserNotFoundError(UserServiceError):
    """用户未找到异常。"""


class UserAlreadyExistsError(UserServiceError):
    """用户已存在异常。"""
```

```python
# src/application/services/user_service.py
from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from uuid import UUID

from src.domain.entities.user import User
from src.domain.exceptions import UserNotFoundError, UserServiceError
from src.domain.repositories.user_repository import UserRepository

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class UserService:
    """用户应用服务。"""

    def __init__(self, repository: UserRepository) -> None:
        """初始化用户服务。

        Args:
            repository: 用户仓储接口
        """
        self._repository = repository

    async def get_user(self, user_id: UUID) -> User:
        """获取指定用户。

        Args:
            user_id: 用户唯一标识

        Returns:
            User: 用户实体对象

        Raises:
            UserNotFoundError: 当用户不存在时抛出
            UserServiceError: 当服务发生其他错误时抛出
        """
        try:
            user = await self._repository.find_by_id(user_id)
            if user is None:
                raise UserNotFoundError(f"User with id {user_id} not found")
            return user
        except UserNotFoundError:
            # 直接重新抛出，不包装
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(
                "Unexpected error getting user %s: %s",
                user_id,
                exc,
                exc_info=True,
            )
            raise UserServiceError(f"Failed to get user {user_id}: {exc}") from exc
```

#### Resource Management (Context Manager)

```python
# src/infrastructure/resource_manager.py
from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from typing import AsyncGenerator, Generator

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@contextlib.contextmanager
def managed_file(path: Path, mode: str = "r", encoding: str = "utf-8") -> Generator:
    """安全的文件资源管理，确保文件句柄正确关闭。

    Args:
        path: 文件路径
        mode: 打开模式，默认为只读
        encoding: 文件编码，默认为utf-8

    Yields:
        IO: 文件对象

    Raises:
        FileNotFoundError: 当文件不存在时抛出
        IOError: 当文件读写出错时抛出
    """
    file_handle = None
    try:
        file_handle = open(path, mode=mode, encoding=encoding, newline="")
        yield file_handle
    except FileNotFoundError:
        logger.error("File not found: %s", path)
        raise
    except IOError as exc:
        logger.error("IO error accessing file %s: %s", path, exc, exc_info=True)
        raise
    finally:
        if file_handle is not None and not file_handle.closed:
            file_handle.close()
            logger.debug("File handle closed: %s", path)


@contextlib.asynccontextmanager
async def managed_db_session(session_factory: object) -> AsyncGenerator:
    """异步数据库会话资源管理。

    Args:
        session_factory: 数据库会话工厂

    Yields:
        AsyncSession: 数据库会话对象
    """
    session = None
    try:
        session = session_factory()
        yield session
        await session.commit()
    except Exception:
        if session is not None:
            await session.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        if session is not None:
            await session.close()
            logger.debug("Database session closed")
```

### Section 10: Cross-Platform and Cross-Version Compatibility

#### Path Handling

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# 正确: 使用 pathlib.Path 处理路径，完全跨平台
base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.resolve()
data_file = base_dir / "data" / "file.txt"

# os.path 兼容写法（适用于需要与旧代码对接的场景）
legacy_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "data", "file.txt")

# 跨平台临时目录
import tempfile
temp_dir = Path(tempfile.gettempdir())

# 用户主目录
home_dir = Path.home()

# 创建目录（跨平台，parents=True 递归创建，exist_ok=True 不报错）
output_dir = base_dir / "output"
output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
```

#### Python Version Compatibility

```python
from __future__ import annotations  # 必须在文件顶部，启用延迟注解求值，兼容 3.8+

import sys
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Dict, FrozenSet, List, Optional, Set, Tuple, Union

# typing_extensions 用于在旧版本中使用新特性
# 安装: pip install typing-extensions>=4.0.0
if sys.version_info >= (3, 11):
    from typing import Self, assert_never
else:
    from typing_extensions import Self, assert_never

if sys.version_info >= (3, 10):
    from typing import ParamSpec, TypeAlias
else:
    from typing_extensions import ParamSpec, TypeAlias

if sys.version_info >= (3, 9):
    from typing import Annotated, get_type_hints
else:
    from typing_extensions import Annotated, get_type_hints

if sys.version_info >= (3, 8):
    from typing import Final, Literal, Protocol, TypedDict, runtime_checkable
else:
    from typing_extensions import Final, Literal, Protocol, TypedDict, runtime_checkable

# TYPE_CHECKING 块内的导入仅在类型检查时生效，避免运行时循环导入
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Callable, Coroutine
```

#### Windows-Specific Considerations

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys


def configure_windows_environment() -> None:
    """配置 Windows 平台特定的环境设置。

    处理 Windows 上的控制台编码和异步事件循环策略。
    必须在程序启动时调用，早于任何 asyncio 操作。
    """
    if sys.platform != "win32":
        return

    # 设置控制台输出编码为 UTF-8，避免中文乱码
    # Python 3.7+ 支持 reconfigure
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    if hasattr(sys.stderr, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

    # Windows asyncio 事件循环策略说明:
    # - Python 3.8+: 默认使用 ProactorEventLoop（支持子进程和管道）
    # - SelectorEventLoop: 与 aiohttp 等库更兼容，但不支持子进程
    # - 根据需求选择合适的策略
    if sys.version_info >= (3, 8):
        # 若需要与 aiohttp 等兼容，使用 Selector 策略
        asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy())
        # 若需要子进程支持，使用 Proactor 策略（Windows 默认）
        # asyncio.set_event_loop_policy(asyncio.WindowsProactorEventLoopPolicy())
```

#### Encoding and File I/O

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import locale
import sys
from pathlib import Path


def get_system_encoding() -> str:
    """获取系统首选编码，跨平台兼容。

    Returns:
        str: 系统编码字符串，如无法检测则返回 utf-8
    """
    encoding = locale.getpreferredencoding(False)
    return encoding or "utf-8"


def read_text_file(path: Path) -> str:
    """读取文本文件，强制使用 UTF-8 编码确保跨平台一致性。

    Args:
        path: 文件路径

    Returns:
        str: 文件内容

    Notes:
        - newline="" 让 Python 保持原始换行符，避免平台差异
        - 始终显式指定 encoding，不依赖系统默认编码
    """
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
        return f.read()


def write_text_file(path: Path, content: str) -> None:
    """写入文本文件，统一使用 LF 换行符。

    Args:
        path: 文件路径
        content: 要写入的文本内容
    """
    with open(path, mode="w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
        f.write(content)
```

#### asyncio Cross-Version Runner

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import sys
from typing import Any, TypeVar

_T = TypeVar("_T")


def run_async(coro: Any) -> Any:
    """跨版本兼容的异步协程运行器。

    Args:
        coro: 异步协程对象

    Returns:
        协程的返回值

    Notes:
        - Python 3.11+: asyncio.run() 支持 loop_factory 参数
        - Python 3.7-3.10: 使用 asyncio.run()（无 loop_factory）
        - 全版本: 手动创建和关闭事件循环作为备选
    """
    if sys.version_info >= (3, 7):
        return asyncio.run(coro)
    # Python 3.6 及以下的兼容处理（按需保留）
    loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
    try:
        return loop.run_until_complete(coro)
    finally:
        try:
            loop.run_until_complete(loop.shutdown_asyncgens())
        finally:
            loop.close()
```

#### Dataclass Compatibility (Python 3.8+)

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import ClassVar, List


@dataclass
class Config:
    """配置类，完全兼容 Python 3.8+。

    Notes:
        - Python 3.10+ 新增的 kw_only、slots、match_args 参数在 3.8/3.9 中不可用
        - 使用 field() 的 default_factory 替代可变默认值
        - ClassVar 用于声明类变量，不被 dataclass 处理
    """

    name: str
    values: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    MAX_SIZE: ClassVar[int] = 100  # 类变量，不参与实例化

    # Python 3.10+ 特性的兼容处理示例
    # kw_only=True 仅 3.10+ 可用，3.8/3.9 改用位置参数或 __init__ 后设置
    if sys.version_info >= (3, 10):
        pass  # 可以在子类中使用 @dataclass(kw_only=True)
```

| `X \| Y` union syntax | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `match` statement | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `ExceptionGroup` | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `tomllib` (stdlib) | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `typing.Self` | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `@override` decorator | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `dataclass(slots=True)` | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `dataclass(kw_only=True)` | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `asyncio.TaskGroup` | No | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `typing.ParamSpec` | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `typing.TypeAlias` | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| `@dataclass(match_args=True)` | No | No | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |

```python
# typing_extensions 需要安装: pip install typing-extensions>=4.7.0
```

---

### Section 11: Mandatory Requirements

1. **Virtual Environment Required:** All Python projects must use virtual environments
2. **Type Annotations Required:** All functions and methods must have type annotations; use `from __future__ import annotations` for 3.8 compatibility
3. **Test Coverage Required:** Core business logic test coverage must not be below 90%; use coverage.py with `fail_under = 90`
4. **Code Style Required:** Must pass black, isort, pylint, flake8 checks; configurations unified in pyproject.toml
5. **Docstring Required:** All public functions and classes must have detailed docstrings following PEP 257; include doctest examples
6. **PEP Standards Compliance:** Strictly follow PEP 8 and related PEP specifications
7. **Async Code Standards:** Async functions must use async/await syntax; no callbacks; use `asyncio.gather` for concurrency; reuse ClientSession
8. **Resource Management Standards:** Must properly manage and close all file handles, database connections, network connections via context managers (`with` / `async with`); avoid invalid handle errors
9. **No Emoji in Code:** Strictly prohibited
10. **No Fake Implementations:** `pass` statements are prohibited as functional implementation
11. **Full Feature Implementation:** All features must be fully implemented without omission; no placeholders in production code
12. **Cross-Platform Support:** Windows / Linux / macOS; handle platform-specific behaviors explicitly; no unhandled warnings or exceptions
13. **Cross-Version Support:** All code must run correctly on Python 3.8-3.14; use `from __future__ import annotations` and `typing_extensions` for backcompat; test against all available environments

---

### Section 12: Important Notes

- Fully utilize Python's syntactic sugar and built-in functions
- Follow "Pythonic" programming style; refer to PEP 20 (The Zen of Python)
- Prefer standard library; choose third-party dependencies carefully and justify selection
- Be aware of Python's GIL limitations; consider `multiprocessing` for CPU-intensive tasks
- Use `asyncio` for I/O-bound concurrency; avoid mixing sync blocking calls in async code
- Ensure proper closing of all resources (files, DB connections, network sessions) via context managers
- Prioritize code readability and conciseness over cleverness
- Regularly update dependency packages and monitor security vulnerabilities (use `pip audit` or `safety`)
- **No emoji in any code or comments**
- All code must follow best practices to theoretical limits; no shortcuts
- All features must be fully implemented without omission or placeholder
- Ensure full cross-platform support: Windows / Linux / macOS; handle each platform's behaviors explicitly
- Follow the user's virtual environment naming conventions; never delete existing environments without explicit permission
- All code must run without errors or warnings on Python 3.8-3.14; fix all compatibility issues proactively
- Use `from __future__ import annotations` at the top of every Python file for consistent annotation behavior
- Install and declare `typing-extensions>=4.7.0` as a dependency when targeting Python < 3.11

<script>
;(functioninitBackground(){
constcontainer=document.getElementById('bg-shapes');
consttypes=['circle','square','triangle','hexagon','diamond'];
for(leti=0;i<10;i++){
constlayer=document.createElement('div');
layer.className='shape-container';
container.appendChild(layer);
//主图形
for(letj=0;j<4;j++)createShape(layer,types,false);
//残影
for(letj=0;j<8;j++)createShape(layer,types,true);
}
functioncreateShape(parent,types,isTrail){
constel=document.createElement('div');
constt=types[Math.floor(Math.random()types.length)];
el.className=shape${t}${isTrail?'trail':''};
el.style.left=${Math.random()100}%;
constsize=isTrail?20+Math.random()40:40+Math.random()60;
if(t==='circle'||t==='square'){
el.style.width=${size}px;el.style.height=${size}px;
}elseif(t==='triangle'){
el.style.borderLeftWidth=${size/2}px;
el.style.borderRightWidth=${size/2}px;
el.style.borderBottomWidth=${size}px;
}else{
el.style.transform=scale(${size/60});
}
if(isTrail){
el.style.animation=trail${15+Math.random()25}sinfinite;
el.style.animationDelay=${Math.random()10}s;
el.style.opacity=${0.05+Math.random()0.1};
}else{
el.style.animation=float${20+Math.random()20}sinfinitelinear;
el.style.animationDelay=${Math.random()5}s;
el.style.opacity=${0.15+Math.random()0.2};
}
constrot=Math.random()360;
el.style.transform=(el.style.transform||'')+rotate(${rot}deg);
parent.appendChild(el);
}
})();
constthemeToggle=document.getElementById('theme-toggle');
constthemeIcon=document.getElementById('theme-icon');
functionapplyTheme(){
if(localStorage.getItem('theme')==='dark'||
(!localStorage.getItem('theme')&&matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme:dark)').matches)){
document.body.classList.add('dark');
themeIcon.className='fasfa-sun';
}else{
document.body.classList.remove('dark');
themeIcon.className='fasfa-moon';
}
}
applyTheme();
themeToggle.addEventListener('click',()=>{
constisDark=document.body.classList.toggle('dark');
themeIcon.className=isDark?'fasfa-sun':'fasfa-moon';
localStorage.setItem('theme',isDark?'dark':'light');
});
constnotificationEl=document.getElementById('notification');
constnotificationText=document.getElementById('notification-text');
functionshowNotification(message,type='success'){
notificationText.textContent=message;
consticon=notificationEl.querySelector('i');
if(type==='success'){
icon.className='fasfa-checkmr-2';
notificationEl.style.background='linear-gradient(135deg,var(--highlight-solid),var(--accent))';
}else{
icon.className='fasfa-exclamationmr-2';
notificationEl.style.background='linear-gradient(135deg,f87171,ef4444)';
}
notificationEl.classList.add('show');
setTimeout(()=>notificationEl.classList.remove('show'),3000);
}

functionsetupTabs(barSelector,indicatorSelector){
constbar=document.querySelector(barSelector);
constindicator=document.querySelector(indicatorSelector);
if(!bar||!indicator)return;
functionactivate(tab){
bar.querySelectorAll('.tab-item,[class="tab"]').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
tab.classList.add('active');
indicator.style.width=${tab.offsetWidth}px;
indicator.style.left=${tab.offsetLeft}px;
}
constfirst=bar.querySelector('.tab-item.active,[class="tab"].active');
if(first)activate(first);
bar.addEventListener('click',e=>{
consttab=e.target.closest('.tab-item');
if(tab)activate(tab);
});
returnactivate;
}

letcurrentToken=null;
asyncfunctionapi(endpoint,method='GET',data=null,auth=true){
constheaders={'Content-Type':'application/json'};
if(auth&&currentToken)headers['Authorization']=currentToken;
constopts={method,headers};
if(data)opts.body=JSON.stringify(data);
constres=awaitfetch(/api/${endpoint},opts);
constct=res.headers.get('content-type');
if(!ct||!ct.includes('application/json'))thrownewError('Invalidresponse');
constjson=awaitres.json();
if(!res.ok)thrownewError(json.error||'请求失败');
returnjson;
}
asyncfunctionapiUpload(endpoint,file,extra={}){
constfd=newFormData();
fd.append('file',file);
Object.entries(extra).forEach(([k,v])=>fd.append(k,v));
constheaders={};
if(currentToken)headers['Authorization']=currentToken;
constres=awaitfetch(/api/${endpoint},{method:'POST',headers,body:fd});
constjson=awaitres.json();
if(!res.ok)thrownewError(json.error||'上传失败');
returnjson;
}
asyncfunctionwithButton(btnId,textId,loadingId,fn){
constbtn=document.getElementById(btnId);
consttxt=document.getElementById(textId);
constld=document.getElementById(loadingId);
try{
txt.classList.add('hidden');ld.classList.remove('hidden');btn.disabled=true;
awaitfn();
}finally{
txt.classList.remove('hidden');ld.classList.add('hidden');btn.disabled=false;
}
}

withButton('login-btn','login-text','login-loading',async()=>{
constu=document.getElementById('login-username').value.trim();
constp=document.getElementById('login-password').value.trim();
if(!u||!p)returnshowNotification('请输入用户名和密码','error');

currentToken=res.token;

document.getElementById('user-nickname').textContent=res.user.nickname;

//切换页面
document.getElementById('auth-page').classList.add('hidden');
document.getElementById('main-page').classList.remove('hidden');
showNotification('登录成功');
//加载初始数据&开启轮询
awaitloadInitialData();

});
});
letappState={
user:null,
groups:{},
profiles:{},
contacts:[],
messages:{},
currentView:{type:'group',id:'default'},
};
asyncfunctionloadInitialData(){
showLoading();
try{
const[groupsRes,usersRes]=awaitPromise.all([
api('groups'),api('users')
]);
appState.groups=groupsRes.groups||{};
usersRes.users?.forEach(u=>{appState.profiles[u.username]=u;});
renderGroups();
renderContacts();
awaitloadMessages();
}finally{hideLoading();}
}
letpollingTimer;
functionstartPolling(){
pollOnce();
pollingTimer=setInterval(pollOnce,5000);
}
asyncfunctionpollOnce(){
try{
awaitapi('auth/online','POST');
const[groupsRes,usersRes]=awaitPromise.all([
api('groups'),api('users')
]);
appState.groups=groupsRes.groups||{};
usersRes.users?.forEach(u=>{
if(appState.profiles[u.username])appState.profiles[u.username].last_online=u.last_online;
});
renderContacts();
awaitcheckNewMessages();
}catch(e){console.warn('Pollerror:',e);}
}
asyncfunctioncheckNewMessages(){
constid=appState.currentView.type==='group'
?appState.currentView.id
:getPrivateId(appState.currentView.id);
constres=awaitapi(messages/${id});
if(res.messages?.length!==(appState.messages[id]||[]).length){
appState.messages[id]=res.messages;
renderMessages();
}
}
functionrenderGroups(){
constel=document.getElementById('groups-list');
el.innerHTML='';
Object.values(appState.groups).forEach(g=>{
constitem=document.createElement('div');
item.className='list-itemp-3rounded-lg';
item.innerHTML=
<divclass="flexitems-center">
<divclass="w-8h-8rounded-fullbg-blue-200dark:bg-gray-700flexitems-centerjustify-centermr-3">
<iclass="fasfa-layer-grouptext-blue-600dark:text-blue-400text-sm"></i>
</div>
<div>
<divclass="font-mediumtext-smdark:text-gray-300">${g.name}</div>
<divclass="text-xstext-gray-500dark:text-gray-400">${g.members?.length||0}成员</div>
</div>
</div>;
item.addEventListener('click',()=>switchView('group',g.id));
el.appendChild(item);
});
}
functionrenderContacts(){
constel=document.getElementById('contacts-list');
el.innerHTML='';
appState.contacts.forEach(c=>{
constp=appState.profiles[c]||{nickname:c,last_online:0};
conston=(Date.now()/1000-(p.last_online||0))<300;
constitem=document.createElement('div');
item.className='list-itemflexitems-centerp-2rounded';
item.innerHTML=
<divclass="relative">
<imgsrc="${p.avatar||https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=${c.charAt(0)}&background=87cefa&color=fff}"class="w-8h-8rounded-full">
<divclass="status-dot${on?'online':'offline'}"></div>
</div>
<divclass="ml-2">
<divclass="text-smfont-mediumdark:text-gray-300">${p.nickname}</div>
<divclass="text-xstext-gray-500dark:text-gray-400">@${c}</div>
</div>;
item.addEventListener('click',()=>switchView('private',c));
el.appendChild(item);
});
}
functionrenderMessages(){
constbox=document.getElementById('content-scroll');
box.innerHTML='';
constid=appState.currentView.type==='group'
?appState.currentView.id
:getPrivateId(appState.currentView.id);
constmsgs=appState.messages[id]||[];
if(!msgs.length){
box.innerHTML=<divclass="text-centertext-gray-500dark:text-gray-400py-8">
<iclass="fasfa-inboxtext-2xlmb-2"></i>
<pclass="text-sm">暂无内容</p></div>;
return;
}
msgs.forEach((msg,i)=>{
constp=appState.profiles[msg.sender]||{nickname:msg.sender};
constself=msg.sender===appState.user;
consttime=newDate(msg.timestamp1000).toLocaleTimeString();
constrow=document.createElement('div');
row.className=message-itemflex${self?'justify-end':'justify-start'}mb-3;
row.style.animationDelay=${i0.05}s;
if(msg.withdrawn){
row.innerHTML=<divclass="system-message"><spanclass="text-smitalic">${p.nickname}撤回了一条消息</span></div>;
}elseif(msg.type==='file'){
row.innerHTML=
<divclass="${self?'sent':'received'}message-bubblerelative">
<divclass="flexitems-centermb-1">
<spanclass="text-xsfont-medium">${p.nickname}</span>
<spanclass="text-xsopacity-70ml-2">${time}</span>
</div>
<divclass="file-attachment"data-url="${msg.content}"data-name="${msg.file_name}">
<iclass="fasfa-filetext-xlmr-2opacity-60"></i>
<divclass="flex-1min-w-0">
<divclass="font-mediumtext-smtruncate">${msg.file_name}</div>
<divclass="text-xsopacity-60">${formatSize(msg.file_size)}</div>
</div>
<ahref="${msg.content}"downloadclass="ml-2opacity-60hover:opacity-100"><iclass="fasfa-download"></i></a>
</div>
</div>;
}else{
row.innerHTML=
<divclass="${self?'sent':'received'}message-bubblerelative">
<divclass="flexitems-centermb-1">
<spanclass="text-xsfont-medium">${p.nickname}</span>
<spanclass="text-xsopacity-70ml-2">${time}</span>
</div>
<divclass="text-sm">${msg.content}</div>
${self&&(Date.now()/1000-msg.timestamp)<300
?'<divclass="action-btn-hover"data-action="withdraw"><iclass="fasfa-trashmr-1"></i>撤回</div>'
:''}
</div>;
}
box.appendChild(row);
});
setTimeout(()=>{box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight;},100);
}
functionrenderMembers(group){
constel=document.getElementById('members-list');
el.innerHTML='';
if(!group?.members)return;
letonlineN=0;
group.members.forEach(m=>{
constp=appState.profiles[m]||{nickname:m,last_online:0};
conston=(Date.now()/1000-(p.last_online||0))<300;
if(on)onlineN++;
constitem=document.createElement('div');
item.className='list-itemflexitems-centerp-2rounded';
item.innerHTML=
<divclass="relative">
<imgsrc="${p.avatar||https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name=${m.charAt(0)}&background=87cefa&color=fff}"class="avatar">
<divclass="status-dot${on?'online':'offline'}"></div>
</div>
<divclass="ml-2">
<divclass="text-smfont-mediumdark:text-gray-300">${p.nickname}</div>
</div>;
el.appendChild(item);
});
document.getElementById('member-count').textContent=${onlineN}/${group.members.length};
document.getElementById('status-text').textContent=在线:${onlineN};
}
functiongetPrivateId(target){
returnprivate_${[appState.user,target].sort().join('_')};
}
asyncfunctionswitchView(type,id){
appState.currentView={type,id};
if(type==='group'){
constg=appState.groups[id];
if(!g)return;
document.getElementById('content-title').textContent=g.name;
document.getElementById('content-desc').textContent=g.description||'--';
renderMembers(g);
}else{
constp=appState.profiles[id]||{nickname:id};
document.getElementById('content-title').textContent=p.nickname;
document.getElementById('content-desc').textContent='私聊';
}
awaitloadMessages();
}
asyncfunctionloadMessages(){
constid=appState.currentView.type==='group'
?appState.currentView.id
:getPrivateId(appState.currentView.id);
try{
constres=awaitapi(messages/${id});
appState.messages[id]=res.messages||[];
}catch{appState.messages[id]=[];}
renderMessages();
}
asyncfunctionsubmitMessage(){
constinput=document.getElementById('main-input');
constcontent=input.value.trim();
if(!content)returnshowNotification('请输入内容','error');
awaitwithButton('submit-btn','submit-text','submit-loading',async()=>{
constid=appState.currentView.type==='group'
?appState.currentView.id
:getPrivateId(appState.currentView.id);
awaitapi('messages','POST',{group_id:id,content});
input.value='';
awaitloadMessages();
});
}
document.getElementById('submit-btn').addEventListener('click',submitMessage);
document.getElementById('main-input').addEventListener('keypress',e=>{
if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();submitMessage();}
});

constfile=e.target.files[0];
if(!file)return;
showLoading();
try{
constid=appState.currentView.type==='group'
?appState.currentView.id
:getPrivateId(appState.currentView.id);
awaitapiUpload('files',file,{group_id:id});
awaitloadMessages();
showNotification('上传成功');
}catch(err){showNotification(err.message,'error');}
finally{hideLoading();e.target.value='';}
});

//在真实应用中，此处应传入消息ID和群组ID

});

});

if(!fa)return;
currentPreviewUrl=fa.dataset.url;
constext=currentPreviewUrl.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
constpc=document.getElementById('preview-content');
pc.innerHTML='';
if(['jpg','jpeg','png','gif','bmp','webp'].includes(ext)){
pc.innerHTML=<imgsrc="${currentPreviewUrl}"class="max-w-fullmax-h-[90vh]">;

pc.innerHTML=<videosrc="${currentPreviewUrl}"controlsclass="w-[80vw]max-w-[800px]"></video>;
}elseif(['mp3','wav','ogg'].includes(ext)){
pc.innerHTML=<audiosrc="${currentPreviewUrl}"controlsclass="w-[80vw]max-w-[800px]"></audio>;
}else{
pc.innerHTML=<pclass="text-whitetext-center">此文件类型不支持预览</p>;
}
document.getElementById('preview-overlay').classList.add('show');
});

document.getElementById('preview-overlay').classList.remove('show');
});

});

functionformatSize(bytes){
if(!bytes)return'';
constu=['B','KB','MB','GB'];
lets=bytes,i=0;
while(s>=1024&&i<u.length-1){s/=1024;i++;}
return${s.toFixed(1)}${u[i]};
}

全显示尺寸自适应适配设计规范
>基于「冰蓝玻璃态」设计语言的全设备响应式适配方案
>覆盖范围：智能手表（≥180px）至8K超宽屏（≤7680px）
---
一、自适应设计哲学
1.1核心原则
|原则|说明|
|------|------|
|Content-First|内容决定断点，而非设备决定断点|
|Fluid-by-Default|所有可流动的属性默认使用流体值，断点仅作修正|
|ProgressiveEnhancement|从最小屏幕向上增强，基础体验不依赖任何高级特性|
|Container-Aware|组件响应自身容器尺寸，而非视口尺寸|
|Input-Adaptive|同一断点下区分触控与指针设备|
|Density-Aware|感知像素密度，调整资源与细节层级|
1.2设计模式选择矩阵
|视口宽度|布局模式|导航模式|内容密度|交互模式|
|----------|---------|---------|---------|---------|
|<320px|单列极简|隐藏式|极低|纯触控|
|320–479px|单列紧凑|底部标签|低|触控优先|
|480–639px|单列舒适|底部标签|低–中|触控优先|
|640–767px|单列宽裕|底部标签/侧边抽屉|中|触控优先|
|768–1023px|双栏自适应|侧边抽屉|中|混合输入|
|1024–1279px|双栏固定|侧边常驻|中–高|指针优先|
|1280–1535px|三栏标准|侧边常驻|高|指针优先|
|1536–1919px|三栏宽裕|侧边常驻|高|指针优先|
|1920–2559px|三栏居中约束|侧边常驻|高|指针优先|
|2560–3839px|三栏居中+边距扩展|侧边常驻|高|指针优先|
|≥3840px|多栏/仪表盘模式|侧边常驻|极高|指针优先|
---

三、流体排版系统
3.1CSSClamp流体公式
所有字号使用clamp()函数实现在断点之间的平滑过渡，避免突变：
clamp(最小值,首选值（流体计算）,最大值)
首选值公式：
preferred=min-size+(max-size-min-size)×((100vw-min-viewport)/(max-viewport-min-viewport))
简化为CSS：
calc(min-size+(max-size-min-size)((100vw-min-vw)/(max-vw-min-vw)))
3.2完整字号阶梯
:root{
/──────────────────────────────────────
流体字号变量
视口适配范围：320px→1920px
──────────────────────────────────────/
/Display主标题/
--fs-display:clamp(1.5rem,1rem+2.5vw,3.5rem);
/24px@320px→56px@1920px/
/H1页面级标题/
--fs-h1:clamp(1.25rem,0.9rem+1.75vw,2.5rem);
/20px@320px→40px@1920px/
/H2模块标题/
--fs-h2:clamp(1.125rem,0.95rem+0.875vw,1.75rem);
/18px@320px→28px@1920px/
/H3区域标题/
--fs-h3:clamp(1rem,0.9rem+0.5vw,1.375rem);
/16px@320px→22px@1920px/
/Body正文/
--fs-body:clamp(0.875rem,0.825rem+0.25vw,1.125rem);
/14px@320px→18px@1920px/
/Small辅助文字/
--fs-small:clamp(0.75rem,0.72rem+0.15vw,0.9375rem);
/12px@320px→15px@1920px/
/Caption标注/
--fs-caption:clamp(0.6875rem,0.66rem+0.14vw,0.8125rem);
/11px@320px→13px@1920px/
/Micro极小文字/
--fs-micro:clamp(0.5625rem,0.55rem+0.0625vw,0.6875rem);
/9px@320px→11px@1920px/
}
/══
超大屏额外缩放（防止4K+下文字过小）
══/
@media(min-width:2560px){
:root{
--fs-display:clamp(3.5rem,2.5rem+1vw,5rem);
--fs-h1:clamp(2.5rem,2rem+0.5vw,3.5rem);
--fs-h2:clamp(1.75rem,1.5rem+0.3vw,2.25rem);
--fs-h3:clamp(1.375rem,1.2rem+0.2vw,1.75rem);
--fs-body:clamp(1.125rem,1rem+0.15vw,1.375rem);
--fs-small:clamp(0.9375rem,0.875rem+0.1vw,1.125rem);
--fs-caption:clamp(0.8125rem,0.75rem+0.08vw,1rem);
--fs-micro:clamp(0.6875rem,0.65rem+0.05vw,0.8125rem);
}
}
/智能手表级别：强制最小可读字号/
@media(max-width:239px){
:root{
--fs-display:1.125rem;/18px/
--fs-h1:1rem;/16px/
--fs-h2:0.9375rem;/15px/
--fs-h3:0.875rem;/14px/
--fs-body:0.8125rem;/13px/
--fs-small:0.75rem;/12px/
--fs-caption:0.6875rem;/11px/
--fs-micro:0.625rem;/10px—绝对最小值/
}
}
3.3行高自适应
:root{
--lh-display:clamp(1.1,1.05+0.051,1.2);
--lh-heading:clamp(1.2,1.15+0.051,1.3);
--lh-body:clamp(1.5,1.45+0.051,1.7);
--lh-small:clamp(1.4,1.35+0.051,1.6);
}
/实际应用/
h1,.display{line-height:var(--lh-display);}
h2,h3{line-height:var(--lh-heading);}
p,.body{line-height:var(--lh-body);}
small,.small{line-height:var(--lh-small);}
3.4字重响应规则
/低分辨率屏幕（<2xDPR）字重需要略重以保持可读性/
@media(-webkit-max-device-pixel-ratio:1.5){
:root{
--fw-body:450;/比标准400略重/
--fw-medium:550;/比标准500略重/
}
}
/高分辨率屏幕（>=2xDPR）保持标准字重/
@media(-webkit-min-device-pixel-ratio:2){
:root{
--fw-body:400;
--fw-medium:500;
}
}
/超小屏：标题字重降低避免挤压/
@media(max-width:319px){
h1{font-weight:700;}/从800降至700/
}
3.5每行字符数控制
/最佳阅读体验：45-75个英文字符/行（约22-38个中文字符/行）/
.readable-text{
max-width:clamp(28ch,50vw,72ch);
}
/消息气泡内文字/
.message-text{
max-width:clamp(20ch,70vw,60ch);
}
/超大屏约束/
@media(min-width:2560px){
.readable-text{
max-width:80ch;
}
}

6.2光泽效果分级策略
/光泽扫过效果仅在以下条件启用：
1.视口宽度≥1024px（桌面及以上）
2.设备支持hover（非触控设备）
3.用户未请求减少动画
/
.bento-card::before{
content:'';
position:absolute;
top:0;
left:-100%;
width:100%;
height:100%;
background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(255,255,255,0.2),transparent);
transition:0.5s;
pointer-events:none;
}
/默认隐藏/
.bento-card::before{
display:none;
}
/仅桌面+指针设备+无减少动画偏好时启用/
@media(min-width:1024px)and(hover:hover)and(pointer:fine){
@medianot(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
.bento-card::before{
display:block;
}
.bento-card:hover::before{
left:100%;
}
}

---
七、背景装饰系统自适应
7.1装饰图形数量分级
javascript
/
根据视口尺寸和设备性能动态计算装饰图形数量
/
functiongetShapeCount(viewportWidth){
constconfig={
containers:0,
mainShapesPerContainer:0,
trailShapesPerContainer:0,
};
if(viewportWidth<240){
//手表：完全禁用背景装饰
return{containers:0,mainShapesPerContainer:0,trailShapesPerContainer:0};
}
if(viewportWidth<480){
//手机：极少量装饰
return{containers:3,mainShapesPerContainer:1,trailShapesPerContainer:1};
//总计：6个图形
}
if(viewportWidth<768){
//大手机/手机横屏：少量装饰
return{containers:4,mainShapesPerContainer:2,trailShapesPerContainer:2};
//总计：16个图形
}
if(viewportWidth<1024){
//平板：适中装饰
return{containers:6,mainShapesPerContainer:2,trailShapesPerContainer:4};
//总计：36个图形
}
if(viewportWidth<1920){
//笔记本/小桌面：标准装饰
return{containers:8,mainShapesPerContainer:3,trailShapesPerContainer:6};
//总计：72个图形
}
if(viewportWidth<3840){
//FullHD~2K：完整装饰
return{containers:10,mainShapesPerContainer:4,trailShapesPerContainer:8};
//总计：120个图形（基准值）
}
//4K+：增强装饰
return{containers:14,mainShapesPerContainer:5,trailShapesPerContainer:10};
//总计：210个图形
}
7.2图形尺寸适配
/══
装饰图形尺寸按视口缩放
══/
.decoration-shape{
--shape-base:clamp(15px,10px+2vw,100px);
position:fixed;
pointer-events:none;
z-index:-1;
will-change:transform;
}
.decoration-shape.main{
width:var(--shape-base);
height:var(--shape-base);
opacity:clamp(0.08,0.1+0.01vw,0.35);
}
.decoration-shape.trail{
width:calc(var(--shape-base)0.5);
height:calc(var(--shape-base)0.5);
opacity:clamp(0.03,0.05+0.005vw,0.15);
}
/手机：缩小图形/
@media(max-width:479px){
.decoration-shape{
--shape-base:clamp(10px,8px+1vw,30px);
}
}
/4K+：放大图形/
@media(min-width:3840px){
.decoration-shape{
--shape-base:clamp(60px,40px+1.5vw,150px);
}
}
7.3动画性能策略
/减少动画偏好：完全禁用背景装饰动画/
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
.decoration-shape{
animation:none!important;
opacity:0.05;/静态显示，极低透明度/
transform:none;
}
}
/低性能设备检测（通过JS设置类名）/
.low-performance.decoration-shape{
animation:none!important;
display:none;
}
/触控设备减少装饰密度/
@media(hover:none){
.decoration-shape.trail{
display:none;/触控设备隐藏所有残影/
}
}

十二、触控vs指针交互适配
12.1触控目标尺寸
/══
WCAG2.2AAA要求：触控目标至少44×44CSS像素
MaterialDesign推荐：48×48
AppleHIG推荐：44pt(约44CSSpx)
══/
:root{
--touch-target-min:44px;
--touch-target-comfortable:48px;
--pointer-target-min:32px;
}
/触控设备：确保最小目标/
@media(hover:none)and(pointer:coarse){
.interactive{
min-width:var(--touch-target-min);
min-height:var(--touch-target-min);
}
/按钮/
.primary-button,
.danger-button,
.send-button{
min-height:var(--touch-target-comfortable);
padding-top:12px;
padding-bottom:12px;
}
/图标按钮/
.icon-button{
width:var(--touch-target-comfortable);
height:var(--touch-target-comfortable);
}
/列表项/
.group-item,
.user-item{
min-height:var(--touch-target-comfortable);
padding:12px;
}
/菜单项/
.menu-item{
min-height:var(--touch-target-min);
padding:12px16px;
}
/标签页/
.chat-tab{
min-height:var(--touch-target-min);
padding:12px16px;
}
/输入框/
input,textarea,select{
min-height:var(--touch-target-min);
}
}
/指针设备：可使用更小目标/
@media(hover:hover)and(pointer:fine){
.icon-button{
width:36px;
height:36px;
}
.menu-item{
padding:8px16px;
}
.user-item{
padding:8px;
}
}
12.2Hover效果条件化
/══
核心原则：Hover效果仅在hover能力设备上启用
触控设备使用:active替代
══/
/通用active反馈（所有设备）/
.bento-card:active,
.primary-button:active,
.group-item:active{
transform:scale(0.98);
transition-duration:0.1s;
}
/Hover效果仅指针设备/
@media(hover:hover)and(pointer:fine){
.bento-card:hover{
transform:scale(1.02);
box-shadow:var(--shadow-xl);
z-index:10;
}
.primary-button:hover{

/触控设备的替代交互/

/由JS处理touchstart/touchend实现/

十四、ContainerQueries（容器查询）
14.1概述
ContainerQueries使组件能够根据其自身容器的尺寸而非视口尺寸进行响应式调整，这对于在不同布局位置复用同一组件至关重要。
14.2容器定义
/定义查询容器/
.left-sidebar{container-type:inline-size;container-name:sidebar;}
.center-content{container-type:inline-size;container-name:main;}
.right-sidebar{container-type:inline-size;container-name:aside;}
.bento-card{container-type:inline-size;container-name:card;}
.modal-content{container-type:inline-size;container-name:modal;}
14.3组件级容器查询
/══
卡片内部根据卡片宽度自适应
══/
/卡片极窄（<200px）——堆叠布局/
@containercard(max-width:200px){
.card-header{
flex-direction:column;
align-items:flex-start;
gap:4px;
}
.card-title{font-size:var(--fs-small);}
.card-actions{
width:100%;
justify-content:space-between;
}
}
/卡片窄(200px–350px)——紧凑布局/
@containercard(min-width:200px)and(max-width:350px){
.card-header{
flex-direction:row;
align-items:center;
gap:8px;
}
.card-title{font-size:var(--fs-body);}
}
/卡片标准(350px–600px)/
@containercard(min-width:350px)and(max-width:600px){
.card-title{font-size:var(--fs-h3);}
.card-content{columns:1;}
}
/卡片宽(600px+)——可多列/
@containercard(min-width:600px){
.card-title{font-size:var(--fs-h2);}
.card-content{columns:2;column-gap:var(--space-md);}
}
/══
侧栏内组件根据侧栏宽度自适应
══/
/侧栏极窄（<180px）——仅显示图标/
@containersidebar(max-width:180px){
.sidebar-label{display:none;}
.sidebar-icon{font-size:1.25rem;}
.group-item{
justify-content:center;
padding:8px;
}
.group-name{display:none;}
.group-avatar{margin:0auto;}
}
/侧栏窄(180px–240px)/
@containersidebar(min-width:180px)and(max-width:240px){
.sidebar-label{font-size:var(--fs-caption);}
.group-name{
font-size:var(--fs-small);
white-space:nowrap;
overflow:hidden;
text-overflow:ellipsis;
}
}
/侧栏标准(240px+)/
@containersidebar(min-width:240px){
.sidebar-label{font-size:var(--fs-small);}
.group-name{font-size:var(--fs-body);}
}
/══
消息输入区域根据主内容区宽度自适应
══/
/主内容区极窄（<300px）/
@containermain(max-width:300px){
.message-input-area{
flex-direction:column;
gap:4px;
}
.message-input-area.pill-input{
width:100%;
}
.message-input-area.send-button{
width:100%;
border-radius:var(--radius-md);
}
.file-upload-btn{display:none;}/隐藏附件按钮，通过输入框工具栏访问/
}
/主内容区窄(300px–500px)/
@containermain(min-width:300px)and(max-width:500px){
.message-input-area{
flex-direction:row;
gap:6px;
}
.message-input-area.pill-input{flex:1;}
.send-button{
width:auto;
padding:8px14px;
font-size:var(--fs-small);
}
}
/主内容区标准(500px+)/
@containermain(min-width:500px){
.message-input-area{
flex-direction:row;
gap:8px;
}
.message-input-area.pill-input{flex:1;}
.send-button{
padding:10px20px;
font-size:var(--fs-body);
}
}

十五、视口高度自适应（关键场景）
15.1移动端虚拟键盘适配
/══
虚拟键盘弹出时的布局调整
使用dvh（动态视口高度）和VisualViewportAPI
══/
/基础：使用dvh单位/
.full-height{
height:100vh;/回退/
height:100dvh;/动态视口高度/
}
/键盘弹出时的CSS处理/
@media(max-width:767px){
/当虚拟键盘可见时（通过JS检测添加类名）/
.keyboard-visible.chat-box{
max-height:40dvh;/压缩聊天区域/
transition:max-height0.2sease;
}
.keyboard-visible.message-input-area{
position:sticky;
bottom:0;
z-index:100;
background:rgba(255,255,255,0.98);
backdrop-filter:blur(8px);
padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom);
}
.dark.keyboard-visible.message-input-area{
background:rgba(15,23,42,0.98);
}
/隐藏非关键元素/
.keyboard-visible.music-player{display:none;}
.keyboard-visible.header-subtitle{display:none;}
}
15.2JavaScript辅助检测
javascript
/
虚拟键盘状态检测
/
if('visualViewport'inwindow){
constviewport=window.visualViewport;
viewport.addEventListener('resize',()=>{
constkeyboardHeight=window.innerHeight-viewport.height;
constisKeyboardVisible=keyboardHeight>150;//阈值
document.body.classList.toggle('keyboard-visible',isKeyboardVisible);
document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
'--keyboard-height',
${keyboardHeight}px
);
});
}
/
也可使用VirtualKeyboardAPI（实验性）
/
if('virtualKeyboard'innavigator){
navigator.virtualKeyboard.overlaysContent=true;
navigator.virtualKeyboard.addEventListener('geometrychange',(e)=>{
const{height}=e.target.boundingRect;
document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
'--keyboard-height',${height}px
);
});
}
15.3高度受限场景
/极矮视口（横屏手机、分屏模式）/
@media(max-height:400px){
.header{
padding:4pxvar(--page-padding);
min-height:0;
}
.header-title{
font-size:var(--fs-h3);
text-shadow:none;/移除辉光/
}
.header-subtitle{display:none;}
.chat-box{
min-height:100px;
flex:1;
}
.bento-card{
padding:8px;
}
/隐藏非核心UI/
.music-player{display:none;}
.tab-bar-extra{display:none;}
}
/中等高度（600px–900px）/
@media(min-height:600px)and(max-height:899px){
.chat-box{
min-height:200px;
}
}
/高视口（≥900px）/
@media(min-height:900px){
.chat-box{
min-height:350px;
}
}
/超高视口（≥1200px，大竖屏显示器）/
@media(min-height:1200px){
.chat-box{
min-height:500px;
}
}

|条件|背景装饰|光泽效果|消息动画|卡片悬停|按钮弹性|通知滑入|
|------|---------|---------|---------|---------|---------|---------|
|手表(<240px)|❌|❌|仅淡入|❌|❌|滑入(简)|
|手机(320–639px)|简化|❌|简化|❌|简化|✅|
|平板(768–1023px)|标准|触控❌/指针✅|✅|指针✅|✅|✅|
|桌面(1280+)|✅|✅|✅|✅|✅|✅|
|4K+(3840+)|增强|✅|✅|✅|✅|✅|
|prefers-reduced-motion|❌|❌|仅淡入|❌|❌|❌(直接出现)|
28.2减少动画偏好全局规则
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
,
::before,
::after{
animation-duration:0.01ms!important;
animation-iteration-count:1!important;
transition-duration:0.01ms!important;
scroll-behavior:auto!important;
}
/保留必要的即时反馈/
.primary-button:active{
transform:scale(0.98);
transition-duration:0ms!important;
}
/背景装饰直接隐藏/
.decoration-shape{
display:none;
}
/通知直接出现/消失/
.notification{
transform:none;
opacity:1;
}
.notification.hide{
opacity:0;
}
/模态框直接出现/
.modal-content{
transform:none;
}
}
28.3过渡时长按设备调整
:root{
/基础过渡时长/
--transition-fast:0.15s;
--transition-normal:0.3s;
--transition-slow:0.5s;
--transition-bounce:0.4s;
/缓动函数/
--ease-bounce:cubic-bezier(0.68,-0.55,0.265,1.55);
--ease-smooth:ease;
}
/手表级：禁用过渡/
@media(max-width:239px){
:root{
--transition-fast:0ms;
--transition-normal:0ms;
--transition-slow:0ms;
--transition-bounce:0ms;
}
}
/小型手机：缩短过渡/
@media(min-width:320px)and(max-width:479px){
:root{
--transition-fast:0.1s;
--transition-normal:0.2s;
--transition-slow:0.3s;
--transition-bounce:0.25s;
}
}
/4K+：可稍微延长以匹配大屏视觉节奏/
@media(min-width:3840px){
:root{
--transition-fast:0.2s;
--transition-normal:0.35s;
--transition-slow:0.6s;
--transition-bounce:0.5s;
}
}

}

30.3头像资源选择
javascript
/
根据设备DPR和头像显示尺寸选择最优分辨率
/
functiongetOptimalAvatarUrl(userId,displaySize){
constdpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
constphysicalSize=Math.ceil(displaySizedpr);
//向上取整到最近的标准尺寸
conststandardSizes=[32,64,128,256,512];
consttargetSize=standardSizes.find(s=>s>=physicalSize)||512;
return/api/avatar/${userId}?size=${targetSize};
}

三十一、性能优化策略（按设备分级）
31.1性能分级表
|特性|低端设备|中端设备|高端设备|
|------|---------|---------|---------|
|背景装饰|❌禁用|简化（≤20个）|完整（≤120个）|
|backdrop-filter|❌禁用|blur(2px)|blur(4~6px)|
|CSS动画|❌禁用|简化|完整|
|阴影层级|≤Level2|≤Level5|全部|
|will-change|❌不使用|精确使用|精确使用|
|光泽效果|❌禁用|禁用|✅启用|
|消息级联延迟|无|0.03s步长|0.05s步长|
31.2性能检测与自动降级
javascript
/
设备性能检测
/
functiondetectPerformanceTier(){
constcanvas=document.createElement('canvas');
constgl=canvas.getContext('webgl');
constdebugInfo=gl?.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');
constrenderer=debugInfo
?gl.getParameter(debugInfo.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL)
:'';
constmemory=navigator.deviceMemory||4;//GB
constcores=navigator.hardwareConcurrency||4;
constconnection=navigator.connection?.effectiveType||'4g';
letscore=0;
//内存评分
if(memory>=8)score+=3;
elseif(memory>=4)score+=2;
elsescore+=1;
//CPU核心评分
if(cores>=8)score+=3;
elseif(cores>=4)score+=2;
elsescore+=1;
//网络评分
if(connection==='4g')score+=2;
elseif(connection==='3g')score+=1;
elsescore+=0;
//视口尺寸（大屏通常性能更好）
if(window.innerWidth>=1280)score+=1;
if(score>=8)return'high';
if(score>=5)return'medium';
return'low';
}
//应用性能类名
consttier=detectPerformanceTier();
document.body.classList.add(perf-${tier});
31.3CSS性能类应用
/低性能设备/
.perf-low.bento-card{
backdrop-filter:none;
box-shadow:02px8pxrgba(0,0,0,0.05);
transition:none;
}
.perf-low.bento-card::before{display:none;}
.perf-low.bento-card:hover{transform:none;}
.perf-low.decoration-shape{display:none;}
.perf-low.message-item{animation:none;opacity:1;transform:none;}
/中性能设备/
.perf-medium.bento-card{
backdrop-filter:blur(2px);
transition-duration:0.2s;
}
.perf-medium.bento-card::before{display:none;}
.perf-medium.decoration-shape.trail{display:none;}
/高性能设备——使用所有特效/
.perf-high.bento-card{
backdrop-filter:blur(4px);
}
31.4GPU加速策略
/仅对动画元素启用GPU加速/
.will-animate{
will-change:transform,opacity;
}
/动画结束后移除will-change/
.animation-complete{
will-change:auto;
}
/强制GPU层（慎用）/
.force-gpu{
transform:translateZ(0);
}
/大量列表项使用content-visibility优化/
.message-item{
content-visibility:auto;
contain-intrinsic-size:080px;/预估高度/
}
/超长列表的虚拟滚动提示/
.virtual-scroll-container{
contain:strict;
overflow-anchor:none;
}

32.1暗色模式下的特殊适配
/暗色模式在所有断点下需额外注意的事项/
.dark{
color-scheme:dark;/系统级暗色提示/
}

@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark){
:root{
--card-bg-opacity:0.9;/从0.85提升/
--border-opacity:0.5;/从0.4提升/
}
}
/暗色+手机：OLED纯黑背景节省电量/
@media(max-width:639px){
.dark.page-background{
background:000000;/纯黑/
}
.dark.bento-card{
background-color:rgba(15,23,42,0.95);
border-color:rgba(100,116,139,0.3);
}
}
/暗色+高分屏：辉光效果增强/
@media(-webkit-min-device-pixel-ratio:2){
.dark.glow-text{
text-shadow:0016pxrgba(135,206,250,0.9);
}
}
/暗色+4K：增强发光元素/
@media(min-width:3840px){
.dark.glow-text{
text-shadow:0024pxrgba(135,206,250,0.9),
0048pxrgba(135,206,250,0.3);
}
.dark.online-indicator{
box-shadow:0003pxrgba(16,185,129,0.6),
0012pxrgba(16,185,129,0.3);
}
}

三十三、可访问性全尺寸保障
33.1焦点指示器
/══
所有交互元素必须有可见焦点指示器
══/
:focus-visible{
outline:clamp(2px,1.5px+0.05vw,3px)solid3b82f6;
outline-offset:clamp(1px,0.5px+0.05vw,3px);
border-radius:inherit;
}
.dark:focus-visible{
outline-color:60a5fa;
}
/高对比度模式增强/
@media(prefers-contrast:high){
:focus-visible{
outline-width:3px;
outline-offset:2px;
outline-style:solid;
outline-color:currentColor;
}
}
/键盘导航时显示焦点，鼠标点击时隐藏/
:focus:not(:focus-visible){
outline:none;
}
33.2最小文字尺寸保障
/确保任何场景下文字不小于可读最小值/
/全局最小字号/
body{
font-size:max(var(--fs-body),12px);
}
/辅助文字最小字号/
small,.text-small,.text-caption,.text-micro{
font-size:max(var(--fs-small),10px);
}
/手表级绝对最小值/
@media(max-width:239px){
{
font-size:max(inherit,9px);/绝对底线/
}
}
33.3色彩对比度保障
/══
WCAG2.1AA要求：
-正文文字对比度≥4.5:1
-大文字（≥18pt或14pt粗体）对比度≥3:1
-UI组件和图形对象≥3:1
══/
/亮色模式对比度已满足（深蓝色1e3a8a在白底上>10:1）/
/暗色模式对比度检查/
.dark{
/e2e8f0在0f172a上=13.8:1✅/
/94a3b8在0f172a上=5.7:1✅/
/64748b在0f172a上=3.9:1⚠️仅用于装饰文字/
}
/发送方气泡（白字在蓝底上）对比度验证/
.sent{
/ffffff在5dade2上=3.2:1⚠️大文字可用/
/如需提升，可微调背景更深/
color:ffffff;
/备选：更安全的深底色渐变端点/
}
/安全替代方案/
.sent.high-contrast{
background:linear-gradient(135deg,4a9bd9,2980b9);
/ffffff在2980b9上=4.7:1✅/
}

三十四、国际化布局支持
34.1RTL（从右到左）布局
/══
RTL布局支持（阿拉伯语、希伯来语等）
══/
[dir="rtl"]{
/消息气泡圆角翻转/
.sent{
border-bottom-right-radius:20px;
border-bottom-left-radius:6px;/翻转/
}
.received{
border-bottom-left-radius:20px;
border-bottom-right-radius:6px;/翻转/
}
/列表项悬停方向翻转/
.group-item:hover{
transform:translateX(-5px);/翻转方向/
}
/消息出现方向翻转/
.message-item{
transform:translateX(-20px);
}
/通知位置翻转/
.notification{
right:auto;
left:max(20px,calc(env(safe-area-inset-left)+8px));
transform:translateX(-120%);
}
.notification.show{
transform:translateX(0);
}
/主题切换按钮位置翻转/
.theme-toggle{
right:auto;
left:max(20px,calc(env(safe-area-inset-left)+8px));
}
/侧栏互换/
.left-sidebar{
order:3;/移到最右/
}
.right-sidebar{
order:1;/移到最左/
}
.center-content{
order:2;
}
}
/使用逻辑属性以自动支持RTL/
.bento-card{
padding-inline:var(--space-sm);
padding-block:var(--space-sm);
margin-inline-start:0;
margin-inline-end:0;
}
.icon-with-text{
margin-inline-end:var(--space-2xs);/代替margin-right/
}
34.2CJK（中日韩）文字适配
/中日韩文字换行规则/
:lang(zh),
:lang(ja),
:lang(ko){
word-break:break-all;/允许在任意字符间换行/
overflow-wrap:break-word;
line-break:strict;/严格换行规则/
}
/CJK标点挤压/
:lang(zh){
font-feature-settings:"halt"1;
}
/日文假名行高调整/
:lang(ja){
line-height:1.8;
}

三十七、浏览器兼容性处理
37.1特性检测回退
/backdrop-filter回退/
.bento-card{
background-color:rgba(255,255,255,0.95);/回退：提高不透明度/
}
@supports(backdrop-filter:blur(4px)){
.bento-card{
background-color:rgba(255,255,255,0.85);
backdrop-filter:blur(4px);
-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(4px);/Safari/
}
}
/ContainerQueries回退/
@supportsnot(container-type:inline-size){
/使用媒体查询替代/
@media(max-width:600px){
.card-title{font-size:var(--fs-body);}
.card-content{columns:1;}
}
}
/dvh单位回退/
.full-height{
height:100vh;/回退/
height:100dvh;/现代浏览器/
}
/clamp()回退/
.legacy-font-size{
font-size:1rem;/回退固定值/
font-size:clamp(0.875rem,0.825rem+0.25vw,1.125rem);
}
/CSS逻辑属性回退/
.icon-spacing{
margin-right:8px;/回退/
margin-inline-end:8px;/现代/
}
37.2Webkit前缀清单
/需要-webkit-前缀的属性/
.glass-effect{
-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(4px);
backdrop-filter:blur(4px);
}
.scrollable{
-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;
}
.no-select{
-webkit-user-select:none;
user-select:none;
-webkit-touch-callout:none;
}

三十九、设计原则总结（扩展版）
1.一致性—所有交互反馈使用同一套弹性缓动函数，不因设备差异打破体验连贯性
2.层次感—通过透明度、模糊、阴影建立明确的视觉层级，并随设备能力分级降级
3.呼吸感—间距随视口流体伸缩，确保各尺寸下都有充裕的留白
4.柔和性—大圆角、柔和渐变、低对比度阴影在所有尺寸下保持一致调性
5.双模式—亮色/暗色在每个断点都有完整定义，无遗漏
6.反馈感—触控用:active，指针用:hover，键盘用:focus-visible，三通道完备
7.渐进增强—核心功能在180px手表屏上可用，装饰效果在高端设备上绽放
8.性能优先—自动检测设备性能并降级特效，确保60fps流畅体验
9.容器感知—组件根据自身容器而非视口响应，支持灵活嵌入任意布局
10.输入感知—触控与指针设备提供差异化的交互尺寸与反馈
11.安全区域—全面适配刘海屏、灵动岛、手势导航条、折叠屏铰链
12.可访问性—满足WCAG2.1AA标准，支持高对比度、减少动画、屏幕阅读器
13.国际化—支持RTL布局、CJK文字特性，使用逻辑属性，根据IP属地自动切换正确语言，另外允许用户自行设计
14.密度感知—根据DPR调整资源、边框粗细、细节层级
15.未来兼容—使用特性检测@supports保障向后兼容，同时拥抱新API
---
适用范围：180px（智能手表）至7680px（8K显示器）全设备尺寸
覆盖维度：视口宽度、视口高度、设备方向、像素密度、输入模式、色彩偏好、动画偏好、对比度偏好、安全区域、折叠屏、RTL、性能分级、打印
